####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-4-1-0

Dependent on a state with an object, a state with an object arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate with an object, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Dependent on a state with an object, a state without an object arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates with an object, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
Dependent on a state with an object, states with an object and states without an object arise because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate with an object, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-1

Dependent on a state without an object, a state without an object arises because of the root condition – dependent on one great element… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a state without an object, a state with an object arises because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates with an object. (2)
Dependent on a state without an object, a state with an object and a state without an object arise because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates with an object, dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-2

Dependent on a state with an object and a state without an object, a state with an object arises because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate with an object and the physical base, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates and…. (1)
Dependent on a state with an object and a state without an object, a state without an object arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates with an object and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
Dependent on a state with an object and a state without an object, states with an object and states without an object arise because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate with an object and the physical base, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates and… dependent on the aggregates with an object and the great elements, matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-3

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-4-2-0

Dependent on a state with an object, a state with an object arises because of the object condition – dependent on one aggregate with an object, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth….
Dependent on a state without an object, a state with an object arises because of the object condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates with an object.
Dependent on a state with an object and a state without an object, a state with an object arises because of the object condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate with an object and the physical base, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates and… (abbreviated). ^7-32-4

####### *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-8-1-0

Dependent on a state with an object, a state with an object arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots and with an object, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots… dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
Dependent on a state with an object, a state without an object arises not because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates without roots and with an object, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots…. (2)
Dependent on a state with an object, states with an object and states without an object arise not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots and with an object, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots…. (3) ^7-32-6

Dependent on a state without an object, a state without an object arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one great element… among non-percipient beings, dependent on one great element…. (1)
Dependent on a state without an object, a state with an object arises not because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates with an object. (2)
Dependent on a state without an object, states with an object and states without an object arise not because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates with an object, dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-7

Dependent on a state with an object and a state without an object, a state with an object arises not because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on one aggregate with an object and the physical base, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates and…. (1)
Dependent on a state with an object and a state without an object, a state without an object arises not because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates without roots and with an object and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots…. (2)
Dependent on a state with an object and a state without an object, states with an object and states without an object arise not because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on one aggregate with an object and the physical base, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates and… dependent on the aggregates with an object and the great elements, matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-8

####### *The Not-Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-8-2-0

Dependent on a state with an object, a state without an object arises not because of the object condition – dependent on the aggregates with an object, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Dependent on a state without an object, a state without an object arises not because of the object condition (as far as the non-percipient beings). (1)
Dependent on a state with an object and a state without an object, a state without an object arises not because of the object condition – dependent on the aggregates with an object and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… (abbreviated). (1) ^7-32-9

With a state with an object as condition, a state with an object arises because of the root condition… three (the same as the dependence section). ^7-32-13

With a state without an object as condition, a state without an object arises because of the root condition – with one great element… with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
With a state without an object as condition, a state with an object arises because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, the aggregates with an object; at the moment of rebirth, with the physical base as condition, the aggregates with an object. (2)
With a state without an object as condition, states with an object and states without an object arise because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, the aggregates with an object, with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-14

With a state with an object and a state without an object as condition, a state with an object arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate with an object and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)

With a state with an object and a state without an object as condition, states with an object and states without an object arise because of the root condition – with one aggregate with an object and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates and… with the aggregates with an object and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
With a state with an object and a state without an object as condition, a state with an object arises because of the object condition – with one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates and… accompanied by body-consciousness… with one aggregate with an object and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth… (abbreviated). (1) ^7-32-16

####### *1-4. Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-26-1-0

Conjoined with a state with an object, a state with an object arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate with an object, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates, two aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. ^7-32-23

The positive method. ^7-32-24

###### The Analysis Section ^7-3-1-9-28-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-29-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-30-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-30-1-0

A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of root condition – roots with an object are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
A state with an object is a condition for a state without an object by way of root condition – roots with an object are a condition, by way of root condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object and a state without an object by way of root condition – roots with an object are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-27

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-30-2-0

A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of object condition – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust arises… distress arises; good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna, the jhāna… noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path, review the fruit, review the abandoned defilements… the suppressed defilements… know the defilements they habitually indulged in before; the aggregates with an object, seen with insight as impermanent… distress arises; by knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness with an object; the base of infinite space for the base of infinite consciousness… the base of nothingness for the base of neither perception nor non-perception… the aggregates with an object are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (1) ^7-32-28

A state without an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of object condition – noble ones review nibbāna. Nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path, for the fruit and for adverting; the eye… the physical base, seen with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness… the aggregates without an object are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (1) ^7-32-29

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-3-1-9-30-3-0

A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, giving it weight, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna, the jhāna… having emerged from the path, the path, giving it weight… the fruit, giving it weight… giving weight to the aggregates with an object, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance with an object is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
A state with an object is a condition for a state without an object by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance with an object is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object and a state without an object by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance with an object is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-32-30

A state without an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – noble ones review nibbāna, giving it weight; nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path and for the fruit; the eye… the physical base, giving it weight, one enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. (1) ^7-32-31

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-30-4-0

A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates with an object… are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition… by way of contiguity condition… one… by way of co-arising condition… seven (in the dependence section, the same as co-arising)… by way of mutuality condition… six (in the dependence section, the same as mutuality)… by way of support condition… seven (in the condition section, the same as support). ^7-32-32

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-3-1-9-30-5-0

A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one gives a gift… fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view. Relying on virtue… wisdom, lust… longing, bodily pleasure… bodily pain, one gives a gift… produces an attainment, kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community. Faith… wisdom, lust… longing, bodily pleasure… bodily pain are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… wisdom, for lust… longing, for bodily pleasure, for bodily pain, for the path and for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state without an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on climate, food and lodging, one gives a gift… produces an attainment, kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community. Climate… food… lodging are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… longing, for bodily pleasure, for bodily pain, for the path and for the attainment of fruition. (1) ^7-32-33

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-3-1-9-30-6-0

A state without an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base, seen with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates with an object. (1) ^7-32-34

####### *The Post-arising and Repetition Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-30-7-0

A state with an object is a condition for a state without an object by way of post-arising condition… one.
A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of repetition condition… one. ^7-32-35

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-3-1-9-30-8-0

A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition with an object is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition with an object is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates. (1)
A state with an object is a condition for a state without an object by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition with an object is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition with an object is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2)
A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object and a state without an object by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition with an object is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition with an object is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates and matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-36

####### *The Result and Nutriment Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-30-9-0

A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of result condition… three.
A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of nutriment condition… three.
A state without an object is a condition for a state without an object by way of nutriment condition – physical food is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body. (1) ^7-32-37

####### *The Faculty Condition* ^7-3-1-9-30-10-0

A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of faculty condition… three.
A state without an object is a condition for a state without an object by way of faculty condition – the material life faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for matter produced by kamma. (1)
A state without an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of faculty condition – the eye faculty for eye-consciousness… the body faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for body-consciousness. (1)
A state with an object and a state without an object are a condition for a state with an object by way of faculty condition – the eye faculty and eye-consciousness are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body faculty and body-consciousness are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness. (1) ^7-32-38

####### *The Jhāna Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-30-11-0

A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of jhāna condition… three, by way of path condition… three, by way of association condition… one. ^7-32-39

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-3-1-9-30-12-0

A state with an object is a condition for a state without an object by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state without an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates with an object. Pre-arising – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for body-consciousness; the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates with an object. (1) ^7-32-40

####### *The Presence Condition* ^7-3-1-9-30-13-0

A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of presence condition – one aggregate with an object is a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
A state with an object is a condition for a state without an object by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (2)
A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object and a state without an object by way of presence condition (the same as the dependence section). (3) ^7-32-41

A state without an object is a condition for a state without an object by way of presence condition – co-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – one great element… (as far as the non-percipient beings). (1)
A state without an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates with an object. Pre-arising – the eye… the physical base, seen with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness… the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates with an object. (2) ^7-32-42

A state with an object and a state without an object are a condition for a state with an object by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and… one aggregate accompanied by body-consciousness and the body base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and… one aggregate with an object and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
A state with an object and a state without an object are a condition for a state without an object by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – the aggregates with an object and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Post-arising – the aggregates with an object and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. Post-arising – the aggregates with an object and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2) ^7-32-43

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-3-1-9-32-2-0

A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of kamma condition. (1)

A state with an object is a condition for a state with an object and a state without an object by way of co-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. (3) ^7-32-45

A state without an object is a condition for a state without an object by way of co-arising condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. (1)
A state without an object is a condition for a state with an object by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition. (2)
A state with an object and a state without an object, for a state with an object – co-arising, pre-arising. (1)
A state with an object and a state without an object, for a state without an object – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. (2) ^7-32-46

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-66-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-66-1-0

A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of root condition – roots that are not consciousness are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of root condition – roots that are not consciousness are a condition, by way of root condition, for consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness by way of root condition – roots that are not consciousness are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-70

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-66-2-0

A state that is consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of object condition – with consciousness as object, consciousness arises. (The base should be worked out.) With consciousness as object, the aggregates that are not consciousness arise. (The base should be worked out.) With consciousness as object, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. (3) ^7-32-71

A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of object condition – after giving a gift, the precepts… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust arises… distress arises; good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna, the jhāna… noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path, review the fruit, review nibbāna. Nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path, for the fruit and for adverting; noble ones review the abandoned defilements that are not consciousness, review the suppressed defilements, know the defilements they habitually indulged in before; the eye… the physical base, the aggregates that are not consciousness, seen with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element. By knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses what is not consciousness; the base of infinite space… the base of nothingness… the visible-form base for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the tangible base for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness… the aggregates that are not consciousness are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (1)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of object condition – after giving a gift… (the same as the first course, without difference; this is the difference) the visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness… the aggregates that are not consciousness for knowledge of supernormal powers… are a condition, by way of object condition, for adverting. (2)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness by way of object condition – after giving a gift… (the same as the first course, without difference; this is the difference), the visible-form base for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the tangible base for body-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the aggregates that are not consciousness for knowledge of supernormal powers… are a condition, by way of object condition, for adverting. (3)
A state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness are a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of object condition – with consciousness and the associated aggregates as object, consciousness arises… three. ^7-32-72

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-3-1-9-66-3-0

A state that is consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – giving consciousness weight, consciousness arises. (1)
A state that is consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving consciousness weight, the aggregates that are not consciousness arise. Co-arising predominance – consciousness-predominance is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state that is consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – giving consciousness weight, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. (3) ^7-32-73

A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, giving it weight, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna, the jhāna… noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path, giving it weight. They review the fruit, giving it weight; they review nibbāna, giving it weight; nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path and for the fruit; the eye… the physical base, the aggregates that are not consciousness, giving them weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is not consciousness is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (1)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of predominance condition (the two courses are the same as the first course, without difference. Object-predominance and co-arising predominance should be worked out). (2)
A state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness are a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of predominance condition… object-predominance (all three should be worked out as having a weighty object, as object-predominance only). ^7-32-74

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-66-4-0

A state that is consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of proximity condition – preceding consciousness is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following consciousness. (1)
A state that is consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of proximity condition – preceding consciousness is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates that are not consciousness; consciousness is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (2)
A state that is consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness by way of proximity condition – preceding consciousness is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following consciousness and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-32-75

A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates that are not consciousness… are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition (these two should be worked out by one who wishes to complete them, like the previous course).
A state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness are a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of proximity condition – preceding consciousness and the associated aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following consciousness. (The base should be asked.) Preceding consciousness and the associated aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates that are not consciousness – consciousness and the associated aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (The base should be asked.) Preceding consciousness and the associated aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following consciousness and the associated aggregates. (3)
By way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of mutuality condition (the same as the dependence section)… by way of support condition… five (the same as the condition section). ^7-32-76

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-3-1-9-66-5-0

A state that is consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – consciousness is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for consciousness… three.
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one gives a gift… fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; virtue… relying on lodging, one gives a gift… causes a split in the monastic community; faith… lodging are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for the path and for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of strong-support condition (these two should be worked out in every case by one who wishes to complete them, the same as the first course, without difference).
A state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness are a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – consciousness and the associated aggregates are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for consciousness… three. ^7-32-77

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-3-1-9-66-6-0

A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base, the aggregates that are not consciousness, seen with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the tangible base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body base… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates that are not consciousness. (1)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base, seen with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for consciousness. (2)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base, seen with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the tangible base…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the body base for body-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for consciousness and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-32-78

####### *The Post-arising and Repetition Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-66-7-0

A state that is consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated). (1)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated). (1)
A state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness are a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated). (1)
A state that is consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of repetition condition… nine. ^7-32-79

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-3-1-9-66-8-0

A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition that is not consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition that is not consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. (1)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition that is not consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition that is not consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for resultant consciousness. (2)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition that is not consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition that is not consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-80

####### *The Result Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-66-9-0

A state that is consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of result condition… five… by way of nutriment condition… five… by way of faculty condition… five. A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of jhāna condition… three… by way of path condition… three… by way of association condition… five. ^7-32-81

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-3-1-9-66-10-0

A state that is consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – aggregates that are not consciousness are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, aggregates that are not consciousness are a condition for matter produced by kamma… aggregates that are not consciousness are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the physical base; the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body base is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates that are not consciousness. Post-arising – aggregates that are not consciousness are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body that arose earlier. (1)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition for eye-consciousness… the body base is a condition for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness. (2)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness and for the associated aggregates. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for eye-consciousness and for the associated aggregates… the body base is a condition for body-consciousness and for the associated aggregates… the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness and for the associated aggregates. (3)
A state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness are a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1) ^7-32-82

####### *The Presence Condition* ^7-3-1-9-66-11-0

A state that is consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). (1)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – aggregates that are not consciousness are a condition, by way of presence condition, for consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for consciousness. Pre-arising – one sees the eye… the physical base with insight as impermanent… (the same as pre-arising, abbreviated). (2)
A state that is not consciousness is a condition for a state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate that is not consciousness is a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, one aggregate that is not consciousness… at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for consciousness and for the associated aggregates. Pre-arising – the eye… the physical base… (the same as pre-arising, abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-83

A state that is consciousness and a state that is not consciousness are a condition for a state that is not consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – one aggregate that is not consciousness and consciousness are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; two aggregates and…. Co-arising – consciousness and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates that are not consciousness (two also at the moment of rebirth). Co-arising – consciousness and the associated aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Co-arising – consciousness and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness (two also at the moment of rebirth). Post-arising – consciousness and the associated aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that arose earlier. Post-arising – consciousness, the associated aggregates and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. Post-arising – consciousness, the associated aggregates and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (1) ^7-32-84

(2)

(3) ^7-32-92

Dependent on a state that is not a mental factor, a state that is not a mental factor arises because of the root condition – dependent on consciousness, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on consciousness, matter produced by kamma; dependent on consciousness, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, consciousness; dependent on one great element… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (2)

(1)
