###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-82-0

With not-root as condition – object: nine, proximity: nine… pre-arising: five, repetition: five, kamma: nine (nine in every case), path: three… non-disappearance: nine. ^7-32-105

###### The Co-arising Section ^7-3-1-9-83-0

(The co-arising section too is the same as the dependence section.) ^7-U385

###### The Condition Section ^7-3-1-9-84-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-85-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-86-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-86-1-0

With a state that is a mental factor as condition, a state that is a mental factor arises because of the root condition… three (the same as in the dependence section).
With a state that is not a mental factor as condition, a state that is not a mental factor arises because of the root condition – with consciousness as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with the physical base as condition, consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, with consciousness as condition, matter produced by kamma; with consciousness as condition, the physical base; with the physical base as condition, consciousness; with one great element as condition…. (1)
With a state that is not a mental factor as condition, a state that is a mental factor arises because of the root condition – with consciousness as condition, the associated aggregates; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates that are mental factors (both to be worked out also at the moment of rebirth). (2)
With a state that is not a mental factor as condition, a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor arise because of the root condition – with consciousness as condition, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; with the physical base as condition, consciousness and the associated aggregates (both to be worked out also at the moment of rebirth). (3) ^7-32-106

With a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor as condition, a state that is a mental factor arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate that is a mental factor and consciousness as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… as condition; with one aggregate that is a mental factor and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… as condition (both to be worked out also at the moment of rebirth). (1)
With a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor as condition, a state that is not a mental factor arises because of the root condition – with the aggregates that are mental factors and consciousness as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with the aggregates that are mental factors and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with the aggregates that are mental factors and the physical base as condition, consciousness (all three to be worked out also at the moment of rebirth). (2)
With a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor as condition, a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor arise because of the root condition – with one aggregate that is a mental factor and consciousness as condition, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; with two aggregates and… as condition; with one aggregate that is a mental factor and the physical base as condition, two aggregates and consciousness; with two aggregates and… as condition (both to be worked out also at the moment of rebirth). (3) ^7-32-107

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-86-2-0

With a state that is a mental factor as condition, a state that is a mental factor arises because of the object condition… three (the same as in the dependence section).
With a state that is not a mental factor as condition, a state that is not a mental factor arises because of the object condition – with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
With a state that is not a mental factor as condition, a state that is a mental factor arises because of the object condition – with the eye base as condition, the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition… with consciousness as condition, the associated aggregates; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates that are mental factors (both to be worked out also at the moment of rebirth). (2)
With a state that is not a mental factor as condition, a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor arise because of the object condition – with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… with the body base as condition… with the physical base as condition, consciousness and the associated aggregates (one at the moment of rebirth). (3) ^7-32-108

With a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor as condition, a state that is a mental factor arises because of the object condition – with one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and eye-consciousness as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… as condition; with one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… as condition; with the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness… with one aggregate that is a mental factor and consciousness as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… as condition; with one aggregate that is a mental factor and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… as condition (two at the moment of rebirth). (1)
With a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor as condition, a state that is not a mental factor arises because of the object condition – with the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness… with the aggregates that are mental factors and the physical base as condition, consciousness (one at the moment of rebirth). (2)
With a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor as condition, a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor arise because of the object condition – with one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base as condition, two aggregates and eye-consciousness; with two aggregates and… as condition; with one aggregate that is a mental factor and the physical base as condition, two aggregates and consciousness; with two aggregates and… as condition (one at the moment of rebirth, abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-109

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-87-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-3-1-9-87-1-0

Root: nine, object: nine, predominance: nine (nine in every case), pre-arising: nine, repetition: nine… non-disappearance: nine. ^7-32-110

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-88-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-89-0

####### *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-89-1-0

With a state that is a mental factor as condition, a state that is a mental factor arises because of the not-root condition – a mental factor without roots (abbreviated).
(The nine questions should be worked out in the same way as for the object condition, also for the five kinds of sense-consciousness, with delusion in the three only. All the questions should be worked out for the course of existence and for rebirth, by one who is not confused.) ^7-32-111

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-90-0

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-91-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-91-1-0

Not-root: nine, not-object: three, not-predominance: nine, not-proximity: three, not-contiguity: three, not-mutuality: three, not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: nine, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: nine, not-kamma: four, not-result: nine, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: nine, not-path: nine, not-association: three, not-dissociation: six, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-32-112

###### Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-92-0

With root as condition – not-object: three, not-predominance: nine (abbreviated). ^7-32-113

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-93-0

With not-root as condition – object: nine, proximity: nine (nine in every case), path: three… non-disappearance: nine. ^7-32-114

###### The Support Section ^7-3-1-9-94-0

(The support section is the same as the condition section.) ^7-U386

###### The Conjoined Section ^7-3-1-9-95-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-96-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-97-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-97-1-0

Conjoined with a state that is a mental factor, a state that is a mental factor arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate that is a mental factor, two aggregates; conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Conjoined with a state that is a mental factor, a state that is not a mental factor arises because of the root condition – conjoined with the aggregates that are mental factors, consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
Conjoined with a state that is a mental factor, a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor arise because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate that is a mental factor, two aggregates and consciousness; conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (3)
Conjoined with a state that is not a mental factor, a state that is a mental factor arises because of the root condition – conjoined with consciousness, the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Conjoined with a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor, a state that is a mental factor arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate that is a mental factor and consciousness, two aggregates; conjoined with two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth…. (1) (Abbreviated). ^7-32-115

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-98-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-3-1-9-98-1-0

Root: five, object: five, predominance: five (five in every case), non-disappearance: five. ^7-32-116

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-99-0

####### *The Exposition* ^7-3-1-9-99-1-0

Conjoined with a state that is a mental factor, a state that is a mental factor arises because of the not-root condition (the five questions should be worked out in the same way, with delusion in the three only. Abbreviated). ^7-32-117

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-100-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-3-1-9-100-1-0

Not-root: five, not-predominance: five, not-pre-arising: five, not-post-arising: five, not-repetition: five, not-kamma: three, not-result: five, not-jhāna: five, not-path: five, not-dissociation: five. ^7-32-118

###### The Association Section ^7-3-1-9-101-0

(The other two counts and the association section should be worked out in the same way.) ^7-U387

###### The Analysis Section ^7-3-1-9-102-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-103-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-104-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-104-1-0

A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of root condition – roots that are mental factors are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of root condition – roots that are mental factors are a condition, by way of root condition, for consciousness and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor by way of root condition – roots that are mental factors are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-119

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-104-2-0

A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of object condition – with the aggregates that are mental factors as object, the aggregates that are mental factors arise. (The base is to be asked.) With the aggregates that are mental factors as object, consciousness arises. (The base is to be asked.) With the aggregates that are mental factors as object, the aggregates that are mental factors and consciousness arise. (3) ^7-32-120

A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of object condition – noble ones, having emerged from the path… review fruit, they review nibbāna. Nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path, for fruit and for adverting; one sees the eye… the physical base, the aggregates that are not mental factors, with insight as impermanent…, enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, consciousness arises. One sees a visible form with the divine eye, one hears a sound with the divine ear element. By knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness that is not a mental factor; the base of infinite space… the base of nothingness… the visible-form base is a condition for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness… the aggregates that are not mental factors are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of the future and adverting. (1)
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of object condition – noble ones, having emerged from the path… review nibbāna (the same as the first course); one sees the eye… the physical base, the aggregates that are not mental factors, with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, one hears a sound with the divine ear element. By knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness that is not a mental factor; the base of infinite space… the base of nothingness is a condition for the base of neither perception nor non-perception… the visible-form base is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the tangible base for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness… the aggregates that are not mental factors are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of the future and adverting. (2)
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor by way of object condition – noble ones, having emerged from the path… review nibbāna (the same as the first course); one sees the eye… the physical base, the aggregates that are not mental factors, with insight as impermanent…, enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. One sees a visible form with the divine eye…; the visible-form base is a condition, by way of object condition, for eye-consciousness and for the associated aggregates… the tangible base for body-consciousness and for the associated aggregates… the aggregates that are not mental factors are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of the future and adverting. (3) ^7-32-121

A state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor are a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of object condition – with the aggregates that are mental factors and consciousness as object, the aggregates that are mental factors arise. (The base is to be asked.) With the aggregates that are mental factors and consciousness as object, consciousness arises. (The base is to be asked.) With the aggregates that are mental factors and consciousness as object, the aggregates that are mental factors and consciousness arise. (3) ^7-32-122

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-3-1-9-104-3-0

A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving weight to the aggregates that are mental factors, the aggregates that are mental factors arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is a mental factor is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. (The base is to be asked.) Object-predominance – giving weight to the aggregates that are mental factors, consciousness arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is a mental factor is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for consciousness and for matter produced by consciousness. (The base is to be asked.) Object-predominance – giving weight to the aggregates that are mental factors, the aggregates that are mental factors and consciousness arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is a mental factor is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-32-123

A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – noble ones, having emerged from the path… review nibbāna, giving it weight; nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path and for fruit; one enjoys and delights in the eye… the physical base, the aggregates that are not mental factors, giving them weight; giving them weight, consciousness arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is not a mental factor is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (1)
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – noble ones, having emerged from the path… giving nibbāna weight… (the same as the first course); one enjoys and delights in the eye… the physical base, the aggregates that are not mental factors, giving them weight; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is not a mental factor is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. (2)
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – noble ones, having emerged from the path… nibbāna… (the first course) one enjoys and delights in the aggregates that are not mental factors, giving them weight; giving them weight, the aggregates that are mental factors and consciousness arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is not a mental factor is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor are a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of predominance condition – object-predominance… three (object-predominance only). ^7-32-124

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-104-4-0

A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates that are mental factors are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates that are mental factors. (The base is to be asked.) Preceding aggregates that are mental factors are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following consciousness. (The base is to be asked.) Preceding aggregates that are mental factors are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates that are mental factors and for consciousness. (3) ^7-32-125

A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of proximity condition – preceding consciousness is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following consciousness; conformity for change-of-lineage… is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for attainment of fruition. (1)
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of proximity condition (the three are to be worked out in this way, the same as the first course. To be completed, without difference).
A state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor are a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of proximity condition… three (there is neither adverting nor emergence).
By way of contiguity condition… nine; by way of co-arising condition… nine (the same as the dependence section); by way of mutuality condition… nine (the same as the dependence section); by way of support condition… nine (the same as the condition section). ^7-32-126

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-3-1-9-104-5-0

A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – aggregates that are mental factors are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates that are mental factors. (The base is to be asked; three kinds of strong-support.) Aggregates that are mental factors are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for consciousness. (The base is to be asked; three kinds of strong-support.) Aggregates that are mental factors are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates that are mental factors and for consciousness. (3) ^7-32-127

A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on climate… food… lodging or consciousness, one gives a gift… causes a split in the monastic community; climate… food… lodging or consciousness are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for wisdom… for lust… for longing, for bodily pleasure, for bodily pain, for the path and for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on climate… food… lodging or consciousness, one gives a gift… (three, the same as the first course, without difference).
A state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor are a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of strong-support condition… three. ^7-32-128

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-3-1-9-104-6-0

A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – one sees the eye… the physical base with insight as impermanent…, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, consciousness arises. One sees a visible form with the divine eye, one hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base is a condition for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for consciousness. (1)
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – one sees the eye… the physical base with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, one hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the tangible base for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body base…. The physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates that are mental factors. (2)
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – one sees the eye… the physical base with insight as impermanent…, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. One sees a visible form with the divine eye, one hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base is a condition for eye-consciousness and for the associated aggregates… the tangible base…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition for eye-consciousness and for the associated aggregates… the body base…. The physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for consciousness and for the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-32-129

####### *The Post-arising and Repetition Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-104-7-0

A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated).
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated).
A state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor are a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated).
A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of repetition condition (abbreviated). ^7-32-130

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-3-1-9-104-8-0

A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition that is a mental factor is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition that is a mental factor is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates. (1)
A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition that is a mental factor is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for consciousness and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition that is a mental factor is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for resultant consciousness and for matter produced by kamma. (2)
A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition that is a mental factor is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition that is a mental factor is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-131

####### *The Result Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-104-9-0

A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of result condition… nine… by way of nutriment condition… nine… by way of faculty condition… nine… by way of jhāna condition… three… by way of path condition… three… by way of association condition… five. ^7-32-132

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-3-1-9-104-10-0

A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – consciousness is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, consciousness is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by kamma; consciousness is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the physical base; the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness. Post-arising – consciousness is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body that arose earlier. (1)
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates that are mental factors. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body base is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates that are mental factors. (2)
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates that are mental factors and for consciousness. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition for eye-consciousness and for the associated aggregates… the body base is a condition for body-consciousness and for the associated aggregates… the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness and for the associated aggregates. (3)
A state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor are a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). ^7-32-133

####### *The Presence Condition* ^7-3-1-9-104-11-0

A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of presence condition… one (the same as the dependence section).
A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (2)
A state that is a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor by way of presence condition… one (the same as the dependence section). (3) ^7-32-134

A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). (1)
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – consciousness is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates that are mental factors; at the moment of rebirth, consciousness…; at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates that are mental factors. Pre-arising – one sees the eye… the physical base with insight as impermanent… (the same as pre-arising, without difference). (2)
A state that is not a mental factor is a condition for a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – consciousness is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, consciousness…; at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates that are mental factors and for consciousness. Pre-arising – one sees the eye… the physical base with insight as impermanent… (the same as pre-arising, without difference). (3) ^7-32-135

A state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor are a condition for a state that is a mental factor by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base are a condition for two aggregates… an aggregate accompanied by body-consciousness…; one aggregate that is a mental factor and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates; two aggregates and…. Co-arising – one aggregate that is a mental factor and consciousness are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates; two aggregates and… (both to be worked out also at the moment of rebirth). (1)
A state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor are a condition for a state that is not a mental factor by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base are a condition for eye-consciousness… for body-consciousness…; the aggregates that are mental factors and consciousness are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness; the aggregates that are mental factors, consciousness and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Co-arising – the aggregates that are mental factors and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for consciousness (all three to be worked out also at the moment of rebirth). Post-arising – the aggregates that are mental factors and consciousness are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that arose earlier. Post-arising – the aggregates that are mental factors, consciousness and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. Post-arising – the aggregates that are mental factors, consciousness and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2)
A state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor are a condition for a state that is a mental factor and a state that is not a mental factor by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates and for eye-consciousness… one aggregate accompanied by body-consciousness and the body base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates and for body-consciousness; two aggregates and…. Co-arising – one aggregate that is a mental factor and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates and for consciousness; two aggregates and… (two to be worked out at rebirth). (3) ^7-32-136

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-113-1-0

Dependent on a state associated with consciousness, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate associated with consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates, one aggregate; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Dependent on a state associated with consciousness, a state dissociated from consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates associated with consciousness, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
Dependent on a state associated with consciousness, a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness arise because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate associated with consciousness, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-144

Dependent on a state dissociated from consciousness, a state dissociated from consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on one great element… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness… at the moment of rebirth… dependent on one great element… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a state dissociated from consciousness, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates associated with consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state dissociated from consciousness, a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness arise because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates associated with consciousness; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-145

Dependent on a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate associated with consciousness and the physical base, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates and…. Dependent on a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness, a state dissociated from consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates associated with consciousness and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates associated with consciousness and the great elements, matter produced by kamma. (2)
Dependent on a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness, a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness arise because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate associated with consciousness and the physical base, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates and… dependent on the aggregates associated with consciousness and the great elements, matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-146

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-113-2-0

Dependent on a state associated with consciousness, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the object condition – dependent on one aggregate associated with consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Dependent on a state dissociated from consciousness, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the object condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates associated with consciousness. (1)
Dependent on a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the object condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate associated with consciousness and the physical base, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates and… (abbreviated). (1) ^7-32-147

####### *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-117-1-0

Dependent on a state associated with consciousness, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the not-root condition – dependent on one aggregate associated with consciousness and without roots, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots… dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
(The nine questions should be worked out in the same way. "Without roots" should be fixed in every case, with delusion in the base term as one only.) ^7-32-149

####### *The Not-Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-117-2-0

Dependent on a state associated with consciousness, a state dissociated from consciousness arises because of the not-object condition – dependent on the aggregates associated with consciousness, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Dependent on a state dissociated from consciousness, a state dissociated from consciousness arises because of the not-object condition – dependent on one great element… (up to non-percipient beings). (1)
Dependent on a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness, a state dissociated from consciousness arises because of the not-object condition – dependent on the aggregates associated with consciousness and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, one (abbreviated). ###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-125-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-125-1-0

With a state associated with consciousness as condition, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the root condition… three (the same as in the dependence section).
With a state dissociated from consciousness as condition, a state dissociated from consciousness arises because of the root condition… one (the same as in the dependence section). (1)
With a state dissociated from consciousness as condition, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, the aggregates associated with consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
With a state dissociated from consciousness as condition, a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness arise because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, the aggregates associated with consciousness; with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-154

With a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness as condition, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate associated with consciousness and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
With a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness as condition, a state dissociated from consciousness arises because of the root condition – with the aggregates associated with consciousness and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
With a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness as condition, states associated with consciousness and states dissociated from consciousness arise because of the root condition – with one aggregate associated with consciousness and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… with the aggregates associated with consciousness and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-155

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-125-2-0

With a state associated with consciousness as condition, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the object condition… one (the same as the Dependence Section). (1)
With a state dissociated from consciousness as condition, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the object condition – with the eye base as condition, the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness… with the physical base as condition, the aggregates associated with consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
With a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness as condition, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the object condition – with one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… with one aggregate accompanied by body-consciousness and the body base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… with one aggregate associated with consciousness and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth… (abbreviated). (1) ^7-32-156

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-127-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-127-1-0

Root: nine, object: three, predominance: nine, proximity: three, contiguity: three, co-arising: nine, mutuality: six, support: nine, strong-support: three, pre-arising: three, repetition: three, kamma: nine… non-disappearance: nine. ^7-32-157

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-128-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-129-0

####### *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-129-1-0

With a state associated with consciousness as condition, a state associated with consciousness arises not because of the root condition… three (the same as the Dependence Section).
With a state dissociated from consciousness as condition, a state dissociated from consciousness arises not because of the root condition – one great element… (as far as non-percipient beings). (1)
With a state dissociated from consciousness as condition, a state associated with consciousness arises not because of the root condition – with the eye base as condition, the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… with the body base… with the physical base as condition, the aggregates without roots, associated with consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… with the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (2)
With a state dissociated from consciousness as condition, states associated with consciousness and states dissociated from consciousness arise not because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, the aggregates without roots, associated with consciousness; with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3)
With a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness as condition, a state associated with consciousness arises not because of the root condition – with one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… with one aggregate accompanied by body-consciousness and the body base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… with one aggregate without roots, associated with consciousness, and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth without roots… with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness and the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (Thus the two questions, in the course of life and at rebirth, are to be worked out. Abbreviated.) ^7-32-158

###### The Conjoined Section ^7-3-1-9-135-0

####### *1-4. Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-135-1-0

Conjoined with a state associated with consciousness, a state associated with consciousness arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate associated with consciousness, two aggregates; conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth….
Root: one, object: one, predominance: one (one in every case), non-disappearance: one.
Not-root: one, not-predominance: one, not-pre-arising: one, not-post-arising: one, not-repetition: one, not-kamma: one, not-result: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-dissociation: one. ^7-32-162

###### The Analysis Section ^7-3-1-9-137-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-138-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-139-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-139-1-0

A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of root condition – roots associated with consciousness are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state dissociated from consciousness by way of root condition – roots associated with consciousness are a condition, by way of root condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness by way of root condition – roots associated with consciousness are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-163

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-139-2-0

A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of object condition – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust arises… distress arises; good deeds done earlier…; having emerged from jhāna, jhāna…; having emerged from the path, noble ones review the path, they review the fruit. They review abandoned defilements… they review suppressed defilements. They know defilements that occurred earlier. They see the aggregates associated with consciousness with insight as impermanent… distress arises. By knowledge of others' minds they know the consciousness of one endowed with a state associated with consciousness. The base of infinite space is a condition for the base of infinite consciousness… the base of nothingness for the base of neither perception nor non-perception… the aggregates associated with consciousness are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (1) ^7-32-164

A state dissociated from consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of object condition – noble ones review nibbāna. Nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path, for the fruit and for adverting; the eye… the physical base; they see the aggregates dissociated from consciousness with insight as impermanent… distress arises; with the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. The visible-form base is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the tangible base… the aggregates dissociated from consciousness are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (1) ^7-32-165

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-3-1-9-139-3-0

A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, giving it weight one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Earlier… jhāna… having emerged from the path, noble ones, giving it weight, review the path, giving it weight, review the fruit; giving weight to the aggregates associated with consciousness, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance associated with consciousness is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state dissociated from consciousness by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance associated with consciousness is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance associated with consciousness is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-32-166

A state dissociated from consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – noble ones, giving it weight, review nibbāna. Nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path and for the fruit; the eye… the physical base, the aggregates dissociated from consciousness – giving them weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. (1) ^7-32-167

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-139-4-0

A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates associated with consciousness… for the attainment of fruition are a condition by way of proximity condition… by way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… seven (the same as the Dependence Section; there are no combinations of questions)… by way of mutuality condition… six (the same as the Dependence Section)… by way of support condition… seven (the same as the Condition Section; there are no combinations of questions). ^7-32-168

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-3-1-9-139-5-0

A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one gives a gift… fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue… longing, bodily pleasure… bodily pain, one gives a gift… kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; faith… bodily pain is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for the path, for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state dissociated from consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on temperature… food… lodging, one gives a gift… causes a split in the monastic community; temperature… food… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for the attainment of fruition. (1) ^7-32-169

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-3-1-9-139-6-0

A state dissociated from consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base as impermanent… distress arises; with the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. The visible-form base is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the tangible base…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body base…; the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates associated with consciousness. (1) ^7-32-170

####### *The Post-arising and Repetition Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-139-7-0

A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state dissociated from consciousness by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated), one; by way of repetition condition… one. ^7-32-171

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-3-1-9-139-8-0

A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition associated with consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. Arising at different moments – volition associated with consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates. (1)
A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state dissociated from consciousness by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition associated with consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Arising at different moments – volition associated with consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2)
A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition associated with consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Arising at different moments – volition associated with consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-172

####### *The Result and Nutriment Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-139-9-0

A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of result condition… three.
A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of nutriment condition… three.
A state dissociated from consciousness is a condition for a state dissociated from consciousness by way of nutriment condition – physical food is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body. (1) ^7-32-173

####### *The Faculty Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-139-10-0

A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of faculty condition… three.
A state dissociated from consciousness is a condition for a state dissociated from consciousness by way of faculty condition – the material life faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for matter produced by kamma. (1)
A state dissociated from consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of faculty condition – the eye faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body faculty…. (2)
A state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness are a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of faculty condition – the eye faculty and the faculty of equanimity are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body faculty and the faculty of pleasure… the body faculty and the faculty of pain are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness. (1)
It is a condition by way of jhāna condition… three… by way of path condition… three… by way of association condition… one. ^7-32-174

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-3-1-9-139-11-0

A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state dissociated from consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state dissociated from consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates associated with consciousness. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body base…; the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates associated with consciousness. (1) ^7-32-175

####### *The Presence Condition* ^7-3-1-9-139-12-0

A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of presence condition… one (the same as the Dependence Section). (1)
A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state dissociated from consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (2)
A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness by way of presence condition (the same as the Dependence Section). (3) ^7-32-176

A state dissociated from consciousness is a condition for a state dissociated from consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). (1)
A state dissociated from consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates associated with consciousness. Pre-arising – the eye… the physical base as impermanent… (the same as pre-arising). (2) ^7-32-177

A state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness are a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base, for two aggregates… one aggregate accompanied by body-consciousness and the body base, for two aggregates… one aggregate associated with consciousness and the physical base, for two aggregates… two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
A state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness are a condition for a state dissociated from consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – the aggregates associated with consciousness and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Post-arising – the aggregates associated with consciousness and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. Post-arising – the aggregates associated with consciousness and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2) ^7-32-178

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-141-0

Root: three, object: two, predominance: four, proximity: one, contiguity: one, co-arising: seven, mutuality: six, support: seven, strong-support: two, pre-arising: one, post-arising: one, repetition: one, kamma: three, result: three, nutriment: four, faculty: six, jhāna: three, path: three, association: one, dissociation: two, presence: seven, absence: one, disappearance: one, non-disappearance: seven.
The positive method. ^7-32-179

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-3-1-9-141-2-0

A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of kamma condition. (1)
A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state dissociated from consciousness by way of co-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. (2)
A state associated with consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness by way of co-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. (3) ^7-32-180

A state dissociated from consciousness is a condition for a state dissociated from consciousness by way of co-arising condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. (1)
A state dissociated from consciousness is a condition for a state associated with consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition. (2)
A state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness, for a state associated with consciousness: co-arising, pre-arising. (1)
A state associated with consciousness and a state dissociated from consciousness, for a state dissociated from consciousness: co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. (2) ^7-32-181

Not-root: seven, not-object: seven, not-predominance: seven, not-proximity: seven, not-contiguity: seven, not-co-arising: six, not-mutuality: six, not-support: six, not-strong-support: seven, not-pre-arising: seven (seven in every case), not-association: six, not-dissociation: five, not-presence: four, not-absence: seven, not-disappearance: seven, not-non-disappearance: four. ^7-32-182

With root as condition – not-object: three, not-predominance: three, not-proximity: three, not-contiguity: three, not-mutuality: one, not-strong-support: three (three in every case), not-association: one, not-dissociation: one, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-32-183

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-145-0

With not-root as condition – object: two, predominance: four (the positive list is to be worked out)… non-disappearance: seven.
The Associated-with-the-Consciousness Pair is finished. ^7-32-184

###### The Conjoined-with-Consciousness Pair ^7-3-1-9-146-0

###### The Dependence Section ^7-3-1-9-147-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-148-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-149-0

Dependent on a state conjoined with consciousness, a state conjoined with consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate conjoined with consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Dependent on a state conjoined with consciousness, a state not conjoined with consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates conjoined with consciousness, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
(The Conjoined-with-Consciousness Pair should be worked out in the same way as the Associated-with-the-Consciousness Pair, without difference.)
The Conjoined-with-Consciousness Pair is finished. ^7-32-185

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-153-1-0

Dependent on a state produced by consciousness, a state produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates… one great element… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a state produced by consciousness, a state not produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates produced by consciousness, consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates produced by consciousness, consciousness and matter produced by kamma. (2)
Dependent on a state produced by consciousness, states produced by consciousness and states not produced by consciousness arise because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness, two aggregates, consciousness and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness, two aggregates, consciousness and matter produced by kamma; dependent on two aggregates…. (3) ^7-32-186

Dependent on a state not produced by consciousness, a state not produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on consciousness, matter produced by kamma; dependent on consciousness, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, consciousness; one great element… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a state not produced by consciousness, a state produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state not produced by consciousness, states produced by consciousness and states not produced by consciousness arise because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates and matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, consciousness and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-32-187

Dependent on a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness, a state produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness and the physical base, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates and…. (1)
Dependent on a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness, a state not produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates produced by consciousness and consciousness, matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates produced by consciousness and the great elements, matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates produced by consciousness and the physical base, consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness, states produced by consciousness and states not produced by consciousness arise because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma; dependent on two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness and the physical base, two aggregates and consciousness; dependent on two aggregates and…. (3) ^7-32-188

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-153-2-0

Dependent on a state produced by consciousness, a state produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Dependent on a state produced by consciousness, a state not produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – dependent on the aggregates produced by consciousness, consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
Dependent on a state produced by consciousness, states produced by consciousness and states not produced by consciousness arise because of the object condition – dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness, two aggregates and consciousness; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-189

Dependent on a state not produced by consciousness, a state not produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, consciousness. (1)
Dependent on a state not produced by consciousness, a state produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state not produced by consciousness, states produced by consciousness and states not produced by consciousness arise because of the object condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, consciousness and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-32-190

Dependent on a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness, a state produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness and the physical base, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates…. (1)
Dependent on a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness, a state not produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates produced by consciousness and the physical base, consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness, states produced by consciousness and states not produced by consciousness arise because of the object condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate produced by consciousness and the physical base, two aggregates and consciousness; dependent on two aggregates…. (3) ^7-32-195

Dependent on a state produced by consciousness, a state not produced by consciousness arises not because of the object condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma. (2) ^7-32-196

Dependent on a state not produced by consciousness, a state produced by consciousness arises not because of the object condition – dependent on consciousness, matter produced by consciousness. (2)

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-165-1-0

With a state produced by consciousness as condition, a state produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition… three (the same as the Dependence Section).
With a state not produced by consciousness as condition, a state not produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… (the same as the Dependence Section). (1)
With a state not produced by consciousness as condition, a state produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – with consciousness as condition, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates produced by consciousness (at the moment of rebirth the two are to be worked out too). (1)
With a state not produced by consciousness as condition, states produced by consciousness and states not produced by consciousness arise because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, consciousness and the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, two (the same as the Dependence Section). (3) ^7-32-201

With a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness as condition, a state produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate produced by consciousness and consciousness as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates… with one aggregate produced by consciousness and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates… (at the moment of rebirth the two are the same as the Dependence Section). (1)
With a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness as condition, a state not produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – with the aggregates produced by consciousness and the physical base as condition, consciousness (at the moment of rebirth all three are to be worked out, the same as the Dependence Section). (2)
With a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness as condition, states produced by consciousness and states not produced by consciousness arise because of the root condition – with one aggregate produced by consciousness and the physical base as condition, two aggregates and consciousness; with two aggregates… (at the moment of rebirth the two are to be worked out, the same as the Dependence Section). (3) ^7-32-202

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-165-2-0

With a state produced by consciousness as condition, a state produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition… three (the same as the Dependence Section).
With a state not produced by consciousness as condition, a state not produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness… with the physical base as condition, consciousness. (1)
With a state not produced by consciousness as condition, a state produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – with the eye base as condition, the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness; with consciousness as condition, the associated aggregates; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates produced by consciousness (the two also at the moment of rebirth). (2)
With a state not produced by consciousness as condition, states produced by consciousness and states not produced by consciousness arise because of the object condition – with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… with the body base… with the physical base as condition, consciousness and the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-203

With a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness as condition, a state produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – with one aggregate produced by consciousness and consciousness as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates… with one aggregate produced by consciousness and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates… (at the moment of rebirth the two are to be worked out). (1)
With a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness as condition, a state not produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – with the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness…; with the aggregates produced by consciousness and the physical base as condition, consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
With a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness as condition, states produced by consciousness and states not produced by consciousness arise because of the object condition – with one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base as condition, two aggregates and eye-consciousness; with two aggregates… accompanied by body-consciousness… with one aggregate produced by consciousness and the physical base as condition, two aggregates and consciousness; with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (3) (Abbreviated). ^7-32-204

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-176-1-0

(2)
