(3) ^7-32-210

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-176-2-0

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of object condition – with the aggregates produced by consciousness as object, aggregates produced by consciousness arise. (The base is to be worked out.) With the aggregates produced by consciousness as object, consciousness arises. (The base is to be worked out.) With the aggregates produced by consciousness as object, aggregates produced by consciousness and consciousness arise. (3) ^7-32-211

A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of object condition – having emerged from the path, noble ones review the path, the fruit… they review nibbāna. Nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path, for the fruit and for adverting; the eye… the physical base, the aggregates not produced by consciousness – one sees them with insight as impermanent… enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, consciousness arises. With the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. By knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one endowed with consciousness not produced by consciousness; the base of infinite space… the base of nothingness… the visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base… the aggregates not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (1)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of object condition – noble ones, having emerged from the path… review nibbāna (the same as the first pass); the eye… the physical base, the aggregates not produced by consciousness – they see them with insight as impermanent… distress arises; with the divine eye one sees visible form… The visible-form base is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the tangible base… the aggregates not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (2)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of object condition – noble ones, having emerged from the path… review nibbāna (the same as the first pass); one sees the aggregates not produced by consciousness with insight as impermanent… enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. With the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. The visible-form base is a condition for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the tangible base… the aggregates not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (3)
A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of object condition… three (to be worked out in the form “with that as object”). ^7-32-212

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-3-1-9-176-3-0

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving weight to the aggregates produced by consciousness, aggregates produced by consciousness arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance produced by consciousness is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness (all three are to be worked out, both object-predominance and co-arising predominance). (3)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of predominance condition – object-predominance – noble ones, having emerged from the path… giving nibbāna weight… giving weight to the aggregates not produced by consciousness, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, consciousness arises. (1)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – noble ones, having emerged from the path… giving nibbāna weight, review it… giving weight to the aggregates not produced by consciousness, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance not produced by consciousness is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – noble ones, having emerged from the path… giving nibbāna weight, review it… giving weight to the aggregates not produced by consciousness… consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. (3)
A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of predominance condition – object-predominance… three (only object-predominance). ^7-32-213

####### *The Proximity and Contiguity Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-176-4-0

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of proximity condition… three (there is no emergence).
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of proximity condition – each preceding consciousness for each following one… for one emerging from cessation, the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition (the other two countings are to be worked out in exactly the same way as this).
A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of proximity condition (three are to be worked out; there is no emergence)… by way of contiguity condition. ^7-32-214

####### *The Co-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-176-5-0

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of co-arising condition (the same as the Dependence Section)… by way of mutuality condition (the same as the Dependence Section)… by way of support condition (the same as the Condition Section). ^7-32-215

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-3-1-9-176-6-0

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – (three questions are to be worked out). (3)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on temperature… food… lodging and consciousness, one gives a gift… causes a split in the monastic community; temperature… food… lodging and consciousness are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for consciousness. (1)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on temperature… food… lodging and consciousness, one gives a gift… causes a split in the monastic community; temperature… food… lodging and consciousness are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for the path, for the attainment of fruition. (2)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on temperature… food… lodging and consciousness, one gives a gift… causes a split in the monastic community; temperature… food… lodging and consciousness are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the aggregates produced by consciousness and for consciousness. (3)
A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support… three. ^7-32-216

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-3-1-9-176-7-0

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of pre-arising condition. Pre-arising object – visible forms produced by consciousness… tangible objects as impermanent… one sees with insight… distress arises; with the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. The visible-form base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the tangible base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness. (1)
A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of pre-arising condition. Pre-arising object – visible forms produced by consciousness… tangible objects as impermanent… one sees with insight, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, consciousness arises. With the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. The visible-form base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for eye-consciousness… the tangible base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for body-consciousness. (2)
A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of pre-arising condition. Pre-arising object – visible forms produced by consciousness… tangible objects as impermanent… one sees with insight, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. With the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. The visible-form base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the tangible base…. (3) ^7-32-217

A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the body, visible forms… tangible objects, the physical base as impermanent… with that as object, consciousness arises. With the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. The visible-form base is a condition for eye-consciousness… the tangible base…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition for eye-consciousness… the body base…; the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for consciousness. (1)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base as impermanent… distress arises. With the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. The visible-form base is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the tangible base…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body base…; the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates produced by consciousness. (2)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base as impermanent… with that as object, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. With the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. The visible-form base is a condition for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the tangible base…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the body base…; the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for consciousness and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-32-218

A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. The visible-form base produced by consciousness and the eye base are a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the tangible base produced by consciousness and the body base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness; the visible-form base produced by consciousness and the physical base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates produced by consciousness… the tangible base produced by consciousness and the physical base…. (1)
A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. The visible-form base produced by consciousness and the eye base are a condition for eye-consciousness… the tangible base produced by consciousness and the body base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for body-consciousness; the visible-form base produced by consciousness and the physical base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for consciousness… the tangible base produced by consciousness and the physical base…. (2)
A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. The visible-form base produced by consciousness and the eye base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the tangible base produced by consciousness… the visible-form base produced by consciousness and the physical base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for consciousness and the associated aggregates… the tangible base produced by consciousness and the physical base…. (3) ^7-32-219

####### *The Post-arising and Repetition Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-176-8-0

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of post-arising condition – post-arising aggregates produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of post-arising condition, for this body produced by consciousness that arose earlier (post-arising should be expanded in just this way)… by way of repetition condition… nine. ^7-32-220

####### *The Kamma and Result Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-176-9-0

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition produced by consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Arising at different moments – volition produced by consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates. (1)
A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition produced by consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Arising at different moments – volition produced by consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for resultant consciousness and for matter produced by kamma. (2)
A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition produced by consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Arising at different moments – volition produced by consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by kamma. (3)
A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of result condition… nine. ^7-32-221

####### *The Nutriment Condition* ^7-3-1-9-176-10-0

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of nutriment condition – nutriments produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (The base is to be worked out.) Nutriments produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… physical food produced by consciousness is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body not produced by consciousness. (The base is to be worked out.) Nutriments produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-222

A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of nutriment condition – at the moment of rebirth, nutriments not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for matter produced by kamma; physical food not produced by consciousness is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body not produced by consciousness. (The base is to be worked out.) Nutriments not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (The base is to be worked out.) At the moment of rebirth, nutriments not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-223

A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of nutriment condition – nutriments produced by consciousness and nutriments not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (The base is to be worked out.) At the moment of rebirth, nutriments produced by consciousness and nutriments not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for matter produced by kamma; physical food produced by consciousness and physical food not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body not produced by consciousness. (The base is to be worked out.) At the moment of rebirth, nutriments produced by consciousness and nutriments not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-224

####### *The Faculty Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-176-11-0

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of faculty condition… three.
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of faculty condition – at the moment of rebirth, faculties not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth… the eye faculty is a condition for eye-consciousness… the body faculty is a condition for body-consciousness… the material life faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for matter produced by kamma. (The base is to be worked out.) Faculties not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… the eye faculty is a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body faculty…. (The base is to be worked out.) At the moment of rebirth, faculties not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by kamma; the eye faculty is a condition for eye-consciousness and for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body faculty…. (3) ^7-32-225

A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of faculty condition – faculties produced by consciousness and faculties not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… the eye faculty and the faculty of equanimity are a condition for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body faculty and the faculty of pleasure… the body faculty and the faculty of pain are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness. (The base is to be worked out.) At the moment of rebirth, faculties produced by consciousness and faculties not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for matter produced by kamma; the eye faculty and the faculty of equanimity are a condition for eye-consciousness… the body faculty and…. (The base is to be worked out.) At the moment of rebirth, faculties produced by consciousness and faculties not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by kamma; the eye faculty and the faculty of equanimity are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates; the body faculty and…. (3)
It is a condition by way of jhāna condition… three… by way of path condition… three… by way of association condition… five…. ^7-32-226

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-3-1-9-176-12-0

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth (abbreviated). (2)
A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition… post-arising (abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-227

A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, consciousness is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by kamma; consciousness is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the physical base; the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition for eye-consciousness… the body base is a condition for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness. Post-arising – aggregates not produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body not produced by consciousness that arose earlier. (1)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (2)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-228

A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (2)
A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition… post-arising (abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-229

####### *The Presence Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-176-13-0

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment (abbreviated). (2)
A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (3)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). (1)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (2)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment (abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-230

A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness… (abbreviated). (1)
A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for eye-consciousness… the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness…. Co-arising – those produced by consciousness… (the same as the Condition Section; both rebirth and the course of life are to be worked out for all the questions.) Post-arising – aggregates produced by consciousness and consciousness are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body not produced by consciousness that arose earlier. Post-arising – aggregates produced by consciousness, consciousness and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body not produced by consciousness. Post-arising – aggregates produced by consciousness, consciousness and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2)
A state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness are a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – that accompanied by eye-consciousness… (abbreviated). (3)
It is a condition by way of absence condition… by way of disappearance condition… by way of non-disappearance condition. ^7-32-231

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-177-0

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-178-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-178-1-0

Root: three, object: nine, predominance: nine, proximity: nine, contiguity: nine, co-arising: nine, mutuality: nine, support: nine, strong-support: nine, pre-arising: nine, post-arising: nine, repetition: nine, kamma: three, result: nine (nine in every case), faculty: nine, jhāna: three, path: three, association: five, dissociation: nine… non-disappearance: nine.
The positive method. ^7-32-232

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-3-1-9-178-2-0

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. (1)
A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition… by way of nutriment condition. (2)
A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. (3) ^7-32-233

A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. (1)
A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition. (2)
A state produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition. (3) ^7-32-234

A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition. (1)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not produced by consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of nutriment condition. (2)
A state not produced by consciousness is a condition for a state produced by consciousness and a state not produced by consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition. (3) ^7-32-235

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-179-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-3-1-9-179-1-0

Not-root: nine, not-object: nine (nine in every case), not-non-disappearance: nine. ^7-32-236

###### Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-180-0

With root as condition – not-object: three, not-predominance: three, not-proximity: three, not-contiguity: three, not-mutuality: two, not-strong-support: three… not-path: three, not-association: two, not-dissociation: three, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-32-237

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-181-0

With not-root as condition – object: nine, predominance: nine (the positive counting is to be worked out)… non-disappearance: nine.
The Produced-by-Consciousness Pair is finished. ^7-32-238

###### The Coexisting-with-Consciousness Pair ^7-3-1-9-182-0

###### The Dependence Section ^7-3-1-9-183-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-184-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-185-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-185-1-0

Dependent on a state coexisting with consciousness, a state coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate coexisting with consciousness, two aggregates and matter coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate coexisting with consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates…. (1)
Dependent on a state coexisting with consciousness, a state not coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates coexisting with consciousness, consciousness and matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates coexisting with consciousness, consciousness and matter produced by kamma. (2)
Dependent on a state coexisting with consciousness, states coexisting with consciousness and states not coexisting with consciousness arise because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate coexisting with consciousness, two aggregates, consciousness and matter produced by consciousness, both coexisting and not coexisting with consciousness; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate coexisting with consciousness, two aggregates, consciousness and matter produced by kamma; dependent on two aggregates…. (3) ^7-32-239

Dependent on a state not coexisting with consciousness, a state not coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on consciousness, matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on consciousness, matter produced by kamma; dependent on consciousness, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, consciousness; one great element… dependent on the great elements, matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a state not coexisting with consciousness, a state coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates and matter coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates coexisting with consciousness; dependent on the great elements, matter coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness and derived matter. (2)
Dependent on a state not coexisting with consciousness, states coexisting with consciousness and states not coexisting with consciousness arise because of the root condition – dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness, both coexisting and not coexisting with consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates and matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, consciousness and the associated aggregates; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, both coexisting and not coexisting with consciousness and derived matter. (3) ^7-32-240

Dependent on a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness, a state coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate coexisting with consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates and matter coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate coexisting with consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate coexisting with consciousness and the physical base, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates and… dependent on the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and the great elements, matter coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness, a state not coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and consciousness, matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and consciousness, matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and the physical base, consciousness; dependent on the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and the great elements, matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (2)
Dependent on a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness, states coexisting with consciousness and states not coexisting with consciousness arise because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate coexisting with consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness, both coexisting and not coexisting with consciousness; dependent on two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate coexisting with consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma; dependent on two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate coexisting with consciousness and the physical base, two aggregates and consciousness; dependent on two aggregates and… dependent on the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter (abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-241

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-186-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-3-1-9-186-1-0

Root: nine, object: nine (all the immaterial is to be extracted; the same as the Produced-by-Consciousness Pair.) Predominance: nine (the great elements are to be worked out in all six questions; in three of the predominance questions there are none.) Proximity: nine, contiguity: nine, co-arising: nine, mutuality: nine, support: nine, strong-support: nine, pre-arising: five, repetition: five, kamma: nine (nine in every case), non-disappearance: nine. ^7-32-242

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-187-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-188-0

####### *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-188-1-0

Dependent on a state coexisting with consciousness, a state coexisting with consciousness arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots, coexisting with consciousness, two aggregates and matter coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots… dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (All nine questions are to be worked out in this way. "Without roots" is to be specified. It is to be worked out as it is obtained in the positive method. The three with delusion are to be worked out just as in the Produced-by-Consciousness Pair.) ^7-32-243

####### *The Not-Kamma Condition* ^7-3-1-9-188-2-0

Dependent on a state coexisting with consciousness, a state coexisting with consciousness arises not because of the kamma condition – dependent on the aggregates coexisting with consciousness, volition coexisting with consciousness.
Dependent on a state not coexisting with consciousness, a state not coexisting with consciousness arises not because of the kamma condition – external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature….
Dependent on a state not coexisting with consciousness, a state coexisting with consciousness arises not because of the kamma condition – dependent on consciousness, the associated volition.
Dependent on a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness, a state coexisting with consciousness arises not because of the kamma condition – dependent on the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and consciousness, the associated volition. ^7-32-244

####### *The Not-Jhāna Condition* ^7-3-1-9-188-3-0

Dependent on a state coexisting with consciousness, a state coexisting with consciousness arises not because of the jhāna condition – that accompanied by the five kinds of sense-consciousness…. ^7-32-245

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-189-0

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-190-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-190-1-0

Not-root: nine, not-object: nine, not-predominance: nine, not-proximity: nine, not-contiguity: nine, not-mutuality: nine, not-strong-support: nine, not-pre-arising: nine, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: nine, not-kamma: four, not-result: nine, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: six, not-path: nine, not-association: nine, not-dissociation: six, not-absence: nine, not-disappearance: nine (the other two countings are to be worked out in the same way). ^7-32-246

(The co-arising section too is the same as the dependence section.) ^7-U394

###### The Condition Section ^7-3-1-9-192-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-193-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-194-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-194-1-0

With a state coexisting with consciousness as condition, a state coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition… three (the same as the Dependence Section).
With a state not coexisting with consciousness as condition, a state not coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, consciousness; with consciousness as condition, matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… (the same as the Dependence Section, all the great elements). (1)
With a state not coexisting with consciousness as condition, a state coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – with consciousness as condition, the associated aggregates and matter coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates coexisting with consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… (all the great elements, the same as the Dependence Section). (2)
With a state not coexisting with consciousness as condition, states coexisting with consciousness and states not coexisting with consciousness arise because of the root condition – with consciousness as condition, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness, both coexisting and not coexisting with consciousness; with the physical base as condition, consciousness and the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth… (the same as the Dependence Section, all the great elements). (3) ^7-32-247

With a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness as condition, a state coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate coexisting with consciousness and consciousness as condition, two aggregates and matter coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; with two aggregates and… with one aggregate coexisting with consciousness and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth… (the same as the Dependence Section, all the great elements). (1)
With a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness as condition, a state not coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – with the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and consciousness as condition, matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; with the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and the physical base as condition, consciousness. With the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and the great elements as condition, matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… (the same as the Dependence Section, all the great elements). (2)
With a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness as condition, states coexisting with consciousness and states not coexisting with consciousness arise because of the root condition – with one aggregate coexisting with consciousness and consciousness as condition, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness, both coexisting and not coexisting with consciousness; with one aggregate coexisting with consciousness and the physical base as condition, two aggregates and consciousness; with two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth… (the same as the Dependence Section, all the great elements). (3) ^7-32-248

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-194-2-0

With a state coexisting with consciousness as condition, a state coexisting with consciousness arises because of the object condition… three (the same as the Dependence Section).
With a state not coexisting with consciousness as condition, a state not coexisting with consciousness arises because of the object condition… with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base…. (This Produced-by-Consciousness Pair is the same as the object condition in the Condition Section. For all six of these, the series based on the five kinds of sense-consciousness are to be worked out. Abbreviated.) ^7-32-249

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-195-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-3-1-9-195-1-0

Root: nine, object: nine, predominance: nine (nine in every case), non-disappearance: nine. ^7-32-250

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-197-0

####### *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-197-1-0

With a state coexisting with consciousness as condition, a state coexisting with consciousness arises not because of the root condition – with one aggregate without roots, coexisting with consciousness…. (Abbreviated. Everything is to be worked out. For the Condition Section, the series based on the five kinds of sense-consciousness are to be worked out for all six as well. With root as condition – not-object: nine (nine in every case), not-kamma: three, not-result: nine, not-association: nine, not-dissociation: five, not-absence: nine, not-disappearance: nine. ^7-32-253

###### The Support Section ^7-3-1-9-202-0

(The support section is the same as the condition section.) ^7-U395

###### The Conjoined Section ^7-3-1-9-203-0

####### *1-4. Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-203-1-0

Conjoined with a state coexisting with consciousness, a state coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate coexisting with consciousness, two aggregates; conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Conjoined with a state coexisting with consciousness, a state not coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – conjoined with the aggregates coexisting with consciousness, consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
Conjoined with a state coexisting with consciousness, states coexisting with consciousness and states not coexisting with consciousness arise because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate coexisting with consciousness, two aggregates and consciousness; conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-255

Conjoined with a state not coexisting with consciousness, a state coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – the associated aggregates conjoined with consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Conjoined with a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness, a state coexisting with consciousness arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate coexisting with consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates; conjoined with two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth… (abbreviated). (2)
Root: five, object: five (five in every case), non-disappearance: five.
The positive method.
Not-root: five (three, delusion), not-predominance: five, not-pre-arising: five, not-post-arising: five, not-repetition: five, not-kamma: three, not-result: five, not-jhāna: five, not-path: five, not-dissociation: five. ^7-32-256

###### The Analysis Section ^7-3-1-9-205-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-206-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-207-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-207-1-0

A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of root condition – roots coexisting with consciousness are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of root condition – roots coexisting with consciousness are a condition, by way of root condition, for consciousness and for matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, roots coexisting with consciousness are a condition, by way of root condition, for consciousness and for matter produced by kamma. (2)
A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of root condition; roots coexisting with consciousness are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by consciousness, both coexisting and not coexisting with consciousness. (3) ^7-32-257

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-207-2-0

A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of object condition… nine (the same as the Produced-by-Consciousness Pair, with no difference). ^7-32-258

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-3-1-9-207-3-0

A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of predominance condition… three (both object-predominance and co-arising predominance are to be worked out).
A state not coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of predominance condition… three (both object-predominance and co-arising predominance are to be worked out for these three as well. All nine questions are the same as in the Produced-by-Consciousness Pair. The last three are object-predominance only). ^7-32-259

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-207-4-0

A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of proximity condition… nine (the same as the Produced-by-Consciousness Pair, with no difference)… by way of contiguity condition… nine (the same as the Dependence Section)… by way of co-arising condition… nine (the same as the Dependence Section)… by way of mutuality condition… nine (the same as the Dependence Section)… by way of support condition… nine (the same as the Condition Section)… by way of strong-support condition… nine (the same as the Produced-by-Consciousness Pair). ^7-32-260

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-3-1-9-207-5-0

A state not coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base… three (only that not coexisting with consciousness is obtained as the base, the same as the Produced-by-Consciousness Pair. All three have no difference). ^7-32-261

####### *The Post-arising and Repetition Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-207-6-0

A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of post-arising condition – post-arising aggregates coexisting with consciousness are a condition, by way of post-arising condition, for this body not coexisting with consciousness that arose earlier. (1)
A state not coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of post-arising condition…. (1)
A state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness are a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated)… by way of repetition condition… nine. ^7-32-262

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-3-1-9-207-7-0

A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition coexisting with consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Arising at different moments – volition coexisting with consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates coexisting with consciousness. (1)
A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition coexisting with consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for consciousness and for matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Arising at different moments – volition coexisting with consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for resultant consciousness and for matter produced by kamma. (2)
A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition coexisting with consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by consciousness, both coexisting and not coexisting with consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Arising at different moments – volition coexisting with consciousness is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates, for consciousness and for matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-263

####### *The Result Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-207-8-0

A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of result condition (the same as the Produced-by-Consciousness Pair)… by way of nutriment condition… nine (the same as the Produced-by-Consciousness Pair. This one too is the same as physical food). A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of faculty condition… nine (the same as the Produced-by-Consciousness Pair, with no difference)… by way of jhāna condition… three… by way of path condition… three… by way of association condition… five. ^7-32-264

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-3-1-9-207-9-0

A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of dissociation condition. Co-arising – aggregates coexisting with consciousness are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness. (1)
A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – aggregates coexisting with consciousness are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Post-arising – aggregates coexisting with consciousness are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body not coexisting with consciousness that arose earlier. (2)
A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of dissociation condition. Co-arising – aggregates coexisting with consciousness are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by consciousness, both coexisting and not coexisting with consciousness. (3) ^7-32-265

A state not coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – consciousness is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, consciousness is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by kamma; consciousness is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the physical base; the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for eye-consciousness… the body base… the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness. Post-arising – consciousness is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body not coexisting with consciousness that arose earlier. (1)
A state not coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – consciousness is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body base… the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates coexisting with consciousness. (2)
A state not coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – consciousness is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by consciousness, both coexisting and not coexisting with consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for eye-consciousness and for the associated matter… the body base… the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-32-266

A state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness are a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of dissociation condition. Co-arising – aggregates coexisting with consciousness and consciousness are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness. (1)
A state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness are a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – aggregates coexisting with consciousness and consciousness are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Post-arising – aggregates coexisting with consciousness and consciousness are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body not coexisting with consciousness that arose earlier. (2)
A state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness are a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of dissociation condition – aggregates coexisting with consciousness and consciousness are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by consciousness, both coexisting and not coexisting with consciousness. (3) ^7-32-267

####### *The Presence Condition* ^7-3-1-9-207-10-0

A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of presence condition – one aggregate coexisting with consciousness… (the same as the Dependence Section). (1)
A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (2)
A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of presence condition – one aggregate coexisting with consciousness… (the same as the Dependence Section). (3)
A state not coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). (1)
A state not coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising (abbreviated. (2)
A state not coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising (abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-268

A state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness are a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness, the eye base and eye-consciousness are a condition for two aggregates… two aggregates and… (everything at rebirth is to be worked out, both co-arising and pre-arising). (1)
A state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness are a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for eye-consciousness… the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness… the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and consciousness are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness. Co-arising – the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for consciousness. Co-arising – the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter not coexisting with consciousness and produced by consciousness (at rebirth all three are to be worked out). Post-arising – the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and consciousness are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body not coexisting with consciousness that arose earlier. Post-arising – the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body not coexisting with consciousness. Post-arising – the aggregates coexisting with consciousness and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2)
A state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness are a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base… (the same as the Condition Section). (3) ^7-32-269

Root: three, object: nine, predominance: nine, proximity: nine, contiguity: nine, co-arising: nine, mutuality: nine, support: nine, strong-support: nine, pre-arising: three, post-arising: three, repetition: nine, kamma: three, result: nine, nutriment: nine, faculty: nine, jhāna: three, path: three, association: five, dissociation: nine, presence: nine, absence: nine, disappearance: nine, non-disappearance: nine.

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-3-1-9-209-2-0

A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of kamma condition. (1)
A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. (2)
A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of kamma condition. (3) ^7-32-271

A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. (1)
A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition. (2)
A state coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition. (3) ^7-32-272

A state not coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition. (1)

A state not coexisting with consciousness is a condition for a state coexisting with consciousness and a state not coexisting with consciousness by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition. (3) ^7-32-273

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-211-0

Not-root: nine, not-object: nine (nine in every case), not-non-disappearance: nine. ^7-32-274

###### Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-212-0

With root as condition – not-object: three, not-predominance: three, not-proximity: three, not-contiguity: three, not-mutuality: three, not-strong-support: three (three in every case), not-association: three, not-dissociation: three, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-32-275

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-213-0

With not-root as condition – object: nine, predominance: nine (the positive list is to be worked out).
The Coexisting-with-Consciousness Pair is finished. ^7-32-276

###### The Accompanying-Consciousness Pair ^7-3-1-9-214-0

###### The Dependence Section ^7-3-1-9-215-0

Dependent on a state accompanying consciousness, a state accompanying consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate accompanying consciousness, two aggregates and matter accompanying consciousness and produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth… (this Pair is to be worked out in the same way as the Coexisting-with-Consciousness Pair, with no difference).
The Accompanying-Consciousness Pair is finished. ^7-32-277

###### The Conjoined-with-and-Produced-by-Consciousness Pair ^7-3-1-9-216-0

###### The Dependence Section ^7-3-1-9-217-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-219-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-219-1-0

Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates, one aggregate; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness, consciousness and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness, consciousness and matter produced by kamma. (2)
Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness, states conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states that are not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arise because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness, two aggregates, consciousness and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-278

Dependent on a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on consciousness, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on consciousness, matter produced by kamma; dependent on consciousness, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, consciousness; one great element… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, states conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states that are not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arise because of the root condition – dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates and matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, consciousness and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-32-279

Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates…. (1)
Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness, matter produced by consciousness; dependent on the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness, matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the great elements, matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base, consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, states conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states that are not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arise because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base, two aggregates and consciousness; dependent on two aggregates… (abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-280

Root: nine, object: nine, predominance: nine, proximity: nine, contiguity: nine, co-arising: nine, mutuality: nine, support: nine, strong-support: nine, pre-arising: five, repetition: five, kamma: nine, result: nine (nine in every case), non-disappearance: nine.
