###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-222-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-223-0

####### *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-223-1-0

Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots, conjoined with and produced by consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots… dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises not because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates without roots, conjoined with and produced by consciousness, consciousness and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots…. (2)
Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness, states conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states that are not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arise not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots, conjoined with and produced by consciousness, two aggregates and consciousness and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots…. (3) ^7-32-282

Dependent on a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises not because of the root condition – dependent on consciousness without roots, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on consciousness, matter produced by kamma; dependent on consciousness, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, consciousness; one great element… (as far as non-percipient beings). (1)
Dependent on a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises not because of the root condition – dependent on consciousness without roots, the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness; dependent on consciousness accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (2)
Dependent on a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, states conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states that are not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arise not because of the root condition – dependent on consciousness without roots, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on consciousness, the associated aggregates and matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on the physical base, consciousness and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-32-283

Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots, conjoined with and produced by consciousness, and consciousness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness…. At the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates… dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness and consciousness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises not because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates without roots, conjoined with and produced by consciousness, and consciousness, matter produced by consciousness; dependent on the aggregates without roots, conjoined with and produced by consciousness, and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness, matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the great elements, matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base, consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, states conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states that are not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arise not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots, conjoined with and produced by consciousness, and consciousness, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on two aggregates… (the two are to be worked out at the moment of rebirth without roots). (3) ^7-32-284

####### *The Not-Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-223-2-0

Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises not because of the object condition – dependent on the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Dependent on a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises not because of the object condition – dependent on consciousness, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on consciousness, matter produced by kamma; dependent on consciousness, the physical base; one great element… (as far as non-percipient beings). (1)
Dependent on a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises not because of the object condition – dependent on the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness, matter produced by consciousness; dependent on the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness (two at the moment of rebirth, abbreviated). (1) ^7-32-285

###### The Condition Section ^7-3-1-9-229-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-230-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-231-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-231-1-0

With a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness as condition, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition (abbreviated) three (the same as the Dependence Section).
With a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness as condition, a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – with consciousness as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with the physical base as condition, consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… (as far as the great elements). (1)
With a state that is not conjoined with and produced by consciousness as condition, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – with consciousness as condition, the associated aggregates; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness (at the moment of rebirth the two are to be worked out) three. (3) ^7-32-289

With a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness as condition, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… with one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates and… (at the moment of rebirth, the two are to be worked out). (1)
With a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness as condition, a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – with the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness as condition, matter not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, with the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the great elements as condition, matter not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, with the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base as condition, consciousness (at the moment of rebirth, all three are to be worked out). (2)
With a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness as condition, states conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arise because of the root condition – with one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness as condition, two aggregates and matter conjoined with and produced by consciousness; with two aggregates… with one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base as condition, two aggregates and consciousness; with two aggregates… (at the moment of rebirth, the two are to be worked out). (3) ^7-32-290

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-231-2-0

With a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness as condition, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition… three (the same as the Dependence Section).
With a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness as condition, a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base… with the physical base as condition, consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
With a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness as condition, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – with the eye base as condition, the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… with the body base… with consciousness as condition, the associated aggregates, with the physical base as condition, the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness (at the moment of rebirth, the two are to be worked out). (2)
With a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness as condition, states conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arise because of the object condition – with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… with the body base… with the physical base as condition, consciousness and the associated aggregates (at the moment of rebirth, one is to be worked out). (3) ^7-32-291

With a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness as condition, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – with one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness, the eye base and eye-consciousness as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates… with one aggregate accompanied by body-consciousness… with one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates… with one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base as condition, two aggregates; with two aggregates… (at the moment of rebirth, two are to be worked out). (1)
With a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness as condition, a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the object condition – with the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness… with the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base as condition, consciousness (at the moment of rebirth, one is to be worked out). (2)
With a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness as condition, states conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arise because of the object condition – with one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base as condition, two aggregates and consciousness; with two aggregates… (at the moment of rebirth, one is to be worked out; abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-292

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-239-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-239-1-0

Conjoined with a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness, two aggregates; conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Conjoined with a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – conjoined with the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness, consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
Conjoined with a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness, states conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states not conjoined with and produced by consciousness arise because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness, two aggregates and consciousness; conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (3)
Conjoined with a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness, a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness arises because of the root condition – conjoined with consciousness, the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-243-1-0

Not-root: five, not-predominance: five, not-pre-arising: five, not-post-arising: five, not-repetition: five, not-kamma: three, not-result: five, not-jhāna: five, not-path: five, not-dissociation: five. ^7-32-299

###### The Association Section ^7-3-1-9-244-0

(In the same way, the other two countings and the association section are also to be worked out.) ^7-U399

###### The Analysis Section ^7-3-1-9-245-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-246-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-247-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-247-1-0

A state conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of root condition – roots conjoined with and produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. (The base should be worked out.) Roots conjoined with and produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of root condition, for consciousness and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (The base should be worked out.) Roots conjoined with and produced by consciousness are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates, consciousness and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-300

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-247-2-0

A state conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of object condition – with the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness as object, aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness arise. (The base should be worked out.) With the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness as object, consciousness arises. (The base should be worked out.) With the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness as object, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. (3)
A state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of object condition – noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path… review nibbāna. Nibbāna is a condition for change-of-lineage…. (Abbreviated. It is to be worked out in the same way as the object condition in the Coexisting-with-Consciousness Pair, without difference. All nine questions.) ^7-32-301

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-3-1-9-247-3-0

A state conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance… three (the two predominances are to be worked out). (3)
A state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance… three (the two predominances are to be worked out). (3)
States conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states not conjoined with and produced by consciousness are a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of predominance condition – object-predominance. (Only one predominance is to be worked out, all nine questions. It is to be worked out in the same way as the Coexisting-with-Consciousness Pair, without difference.) ^7-32-302

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-247-4-0

A state conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of proximity condition (all nine questions are the same as the Coexisting-with-Consciousness Pair)… by way of contiguity condition… nine… by way of co-arising condition… nine (the same as the Dependence Section)… by way of mutuality condition… nine (the same as the Dependence Section)… by way of support condition… nine (the same as the Condition Section)… by way of strong-support condition (all nine questions are the same as the Coexisting-with-Consciousness Pair, without difference). ^7-32-303

####### *The Pre-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-247-5-0

A state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base… three (the same as the Coexisting-with-Consciousness Pair, without difference).
A state conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of post-arising condition (the same as the Coexisting-with-Consciousness Pair, without difference. All three are post-arising. Two. For those with one base, one combination)… by way of repetition condition… nine. ^7-32-304

####### *The Kamma Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-247-6-0

A state conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of kamma condition… three (the same as the Coexisting-with-Consciousness Pair, without difference. All three are co-arising and from a different moment)… by way of result condition… nine… by way of nutriment condition… nine (the same as the course for the Coexisting-with-Consciousness Pair, with physical food as the only nutriment)… by way of faculty condition… nine… by way of jhāna condition… three… by way of path condition… three… by way of association condition… five. ^7-32-305

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-3-1-9-247-7-0

A state conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body base… the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness. (2)
A state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness and the associated aggregates. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the body base… the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness and the associated aggregates. (3)
States conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states not conjoined with and produced by consciousness are a condition for a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). ^7-32-306

####### *The Presence Condition* ^7-3-1-9-247-8-0

A state conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of presence condition (the same as the Dependence Section). A state conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). A state conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of presence condition (the same as the Dependence Section). (3)
A state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (the pre-arising is to be worked out in the same way as pre-arising. Everything is abbreviated. It should be expanded). (1)
A state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – consciousness is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, consciousness is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the associated aggregates, and at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness. Pre-arising (the same as pre-arising, without difference). (2)
A state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness is a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – consciousness is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, consciousness is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by kamma, and at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for consciousness and the associated aggregates. Pre-arising (the same as pre-arising). (3) ^7-32-307

States conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states not conjoined with and produced by consciousness are a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and eye-consciousness are a condition for two aggregates… two aggregates and… one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base are a condition for two aggregates… two aggregates and… one aggregate accompanied by body-consciousness… one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates; two aggregates and… one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates… two aggregates and… (at the moment of rebirth, the two are to be worked out). (1)
States conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states not conjoined with and produced by consciousness are a condition for a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for eye-consciousness… the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness… the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter conjoined with and produced by consciousness, the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter conjoined with and produced by consciousness, the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for consciousness (at the moment of rebirth, all three are to be worked out). Post-arising – the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body not conjoined with and produced by consciousness that arose earlier. Post-arising – the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness, consciousness and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body not conjoined with and produced by consciousness. Post-arising – the aggregates conjoined with and produced by consciousness, consciousness and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2)
States conjoined with and produced by consciousness and states not conjoined with and produced by consciousness are a condition for a state conjoined with and produced by consciousness and a state not conjoined with and produced by consciousness by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates and eye-consciousness, two aggregates and… one aggregate accompanied by body-consciousness…. Co-arising – one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and consciousness are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness, two aggregates and… one aggregate conjoined with and produced by consciousness and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates and consciousness, two aggregates and… (at the moment of rebirth, the two are to be worked out). (3) ^7-32-308

####### *1-4. Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-278-1-0

Conjoined with an internal state, an external state arises because of the root condition – conjoined with consciousness, the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, conjoined with consciousness, the associated aggregates. (1)
Conjoined with an external state, an external state arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one external aggregate, two aggregates; conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. Conjoined with an external state, an internal state arises because of the root condition – conjoined with the external aggregates, consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3)

Conjoined with an internal state, an external state arises not because of the root condition (in this way five are to be worked out, with delusion in the three only).
Not-root: five, not-predominance: five, not-pre-arising: five, not-post-arising: five, not-repetition: five, not-kamma: three, not-result: five, not-jhāna: five, not-path: five, not-dissociation: five (the negative method). ^7-32-339

###### The Association Section ^7-3-1-9-279-0

(In the same way, the other two countings and the association section are also to be worked out.) ^7-U402

###### The Analysis Section ^7-3-1-9-280-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-281-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-282-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-282-1-0

An external state is a condition for an external state by way of root condition – external roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness – at the moment of rebirth, external roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and external matter produced by kamma. (1)
An external state is a condition for an internal state by way of root condition – external roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, external roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for consciousness and internal matter produced by kamma. (2)
An external state is a condition for an internal state and an external state by way of root condition – external roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates, consciousness and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, external roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates, consciousness and internal and external matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-340

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-282-2-0

An internal state is a condition for an internal state by way of object condition – with consciousness as object, consciousness arises. (The base is to be asked.) With consciousness as object, the external aggregates arise. (The base is to be asked.) With consciousness as object, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. (3)
An external state is a condition for an external state by way of object condition – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust… distress arises; one reviews good deeds done in the past, having emerged from jhāna, the jhāna… noble ones, having emerged from the path, the path… the fruit… review nibbāna… Nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path, for the fruit and for adverting. Noble ones review the abandoned external defilements, the suppressed defilements… know the defilements they habitually indulged in before, visible forms… the physical base, the external aggregates, seen with insight as impermanent… distress arises; with the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. By knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses external consciousness, the base of infinite space for the base of infinite consciousness… the base of nothingness for the base of neither perception nor non-perception… the visible-form base is a condition, by way of object condition, for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the tangible base…; the external aggregates are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (1)
An external state is a condition for an internal state by way of object condition – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, consciousness arises. Good deeds done in the past… the jhāna… (abbreviated, everything is to be worked out) the defilements they habitually indulged in before… visible forms… the physical base, the external aggregates as impermanent… one sees with insight, enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, consciousness arises. With the divine eye one sees visible form… the visible-form base is a condition, by way of object condition, for eye-consciousness… the tangible base…; the external aggregates are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (3)
An external state is a condition for an internal state and an external state by way of object condition – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise (abbreviated, everything is to be worked out). One sees the external aggregates with insight as impermanent… enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. With the divine eye one sees visible form… the visible-form base is a condition, by way of object condition, for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the tangible base…; the external aggregates are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (3)
An internal state and an external state are a condition for an internal state by way of object condition… three. ^7-32-341

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-3-1-9-282-3-0

An internal state is a condition for an internal state by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – giving consciousness weight, consciousness arises. (1)
An internal state is a condition for an external state by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving consciousness weight, the external aggregates arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance of consciousness is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (The base.) Object-predominance – giving internal consciousness weight, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. (3)
An external state is a condition for an external state by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – having given a gift… three (the two predominances are to be worked out for all three). (3)
An internal state and an external state are a condition for an internal state by way of predominance condition… three (for all three, only one predominance). ^7-32-342

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-282-4-0

An internal state is a condition for an internal state by way of proximity condition – each preceding consciousness is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for each following consciousness… three.
An external state is a condition for an external state by way of proximity condition – each preceding external aggregate is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for each following aggregate; conformity for change-of-lineage… three (all three alike).
An internal state and an external state are a condition for an internal state by way of proximity condition… three, by way of contiguity condition… nine, by way of co-arising condition… nine (the same as the Dependence Section), by way of mutuality condition… five (the same as the Dependence Section), by way of support condition… nine (the same as the Condition Section). ^7-32-343

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-3-1-9-282-5-0

An internal state is a condition for an internal state by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – consciousness is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for consciousness… three.
An external state is a condition for an external state by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one gives a gift… fosters conceit, takes up wrong view; virtue… relying on lodging, one gives a gift… causes a split in the monastic community; faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for the attainment of fruition (all three are to be completed and worked out; 'for consciousness' is to be worked out, and 'for the associated aggregates' is to be worked out).
An internal state and an external state are a condition for an internal state by way of strong-support condition… three. ^7-32-344

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-3-1-9-282-6-0

An internal state is a condition for an internal state by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the body as impermanent… one sees with insight, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, consciousness arises. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for eye-consciousness… the body base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for body-consciousness. (1)
An internal state is a condition for an external state by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the body as impermanent… one sees with insight, enjoys… distress arises. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness. (2)
An internal state is a condition for an internal state and an external state by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the body as impermanent… one sees with insight; with that as object, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the body base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for body-consciousness and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-32-345

An external state is a condition for an external state by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – visible forms… tangible objects… the physical base as impermanent… distress arises; with the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the external aggregates. (1)
An external state is a condition for an internal state by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – visible forms… tangible objects… the physical base as impermanent… with that as object, consciousness arises. With the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for consciousness. (2)
An external state is a condition for an internal state and an external state by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – visible forms… tangible objects… the physical base as impermanent… with that as object, consciousness and the associated aggregates arise. With the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for consciousness and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-32-346

An internal state and an external state are a condition for an internal state by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. The eye base and the physical base are a condition for consciousness… the body base and the physical base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for consciousness; the visible-form base and the eye base are a condition for eye-consciousness… the tangible base and the body base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for body-consciousness. (1)
An internal state and an external state are a condition for an external state by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. The eye base and the physical base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the external aggregates… the body base and the physical base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the external aggregates; the visible-form base and the eye base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the tangible base and the body base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness. (2)
An internal state and an external state are a condition for an internal state and an external state by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. The eye base and the physical base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for consciousness and the associated aggregates… the body base and the physical base… the visible-form base and the eye base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the tangible base and…. (3) ^7-32-347

####### *The Post-arising and Repetition Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-282-7-0

An internal state is a condition for an internal state by way of post-arising condition – post-arising internal aggregates are a condition, by way of post-arising condition, for this internal body that arose earlier. (The base should be worked out) post-arising internal aggregates are a condition, by way of post-arising condition, for this external body that arose earlier. (The base should be worked out) post-arising internal aggregates are a condition, by way of post-arising condition, for this internal and external body that arose earlier (in the same way all nine questions should be worked out); by way of repetition condition (nine questions should be worked out). ^7-32-348

####### *The Kamma and Result Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-282-8-0

An external state is a condition for an external state by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – external volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – external volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant external aggregates and matter produced by kamma. (1)
An external state is a condition for an internal state by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – external volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – external volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for resultant consciousness and internal matter produced by kamma. (2)
An external state is a condition for an internal and an external state by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – external volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates, consciousness and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – external volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates, consciousness, and internal and external matter produced by kamma. (3) By way of result condition… nine. ^7-32-349

####### *The Nutriment Condition* ^7-3-1-9-282-9-0

An internal state is a condition for an internal state by way of nutriment condition – at the moment of rebirth, internal nutriments are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for internal matter produced by kamma. (1)
An internal state is a condition for an external state by way of nutriment condition – internal nutriments are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, internal nutriments are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and external matter produced by kamma. (The base should be worked out) at the moment of rebirth, internal nutriments are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and internal and external matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-350

An external state is a condition for an external state by way of nutriment condition – external nutriments are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… external physical food is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the external body. (1)
An external state is a condition for an internal state by way of nutriment condition – external nutriments are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, external nutriments are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for consciousness and internal matter produced by kamma; external physical food is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the internal body. (2)
An external state is a condition for an internal and an external state by way of nutriment condition – external nutriments are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates, consciousness and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, external nutriments are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates, consciousness, and internal and external matter produced by kamma; external physical food is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the internal and external body. (3) ^7-32-351

Internal and external states are a condition for an internal state by way of nutriment condition – at the moment of rebirth, internal and external nutriments are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for internal matter produced by kamma. (1)
Internal and external states are a condition for an external state by way of nutriment condition – internal and external nutriments are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, internal and external nutriments are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and external matter produced by kamma. (2)
Internal and external states are a condition for an internal and an external state by way of nutriment condition – at the moment of rebirth, internal and external nutriments are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and internal and external matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-352

####### *The Faculty Condition* ^7-3-1-9-282-10-0

An internal state is a condition for an internal state by way of faculty condition – at the moment of rebirth, internal faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for internal matter produced by kamma; the eye faculty for eye-consciousness… the body faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for body-consciousness. (1)
An internal state is a condition for an external state by way of faculty condition – internal faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, internal faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and external matter produced by kamma; the eye faculty for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness. (2)
An internal state is a condition for an internal and an external state by way of faculty condition – at the moment of rebirth, internal faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and internal and external matter produced by kamma; the eye faculty for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the body faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for body-consciousness and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-32-353

An external state is a condition for an external state by way of faculty condition – external faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, external faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and external matter produced by kamma; the material life faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for external matter produced by kamma. (1)
An external state is a condition for an internal state by way of faculty condition – external faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, external faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for consciousness and internal matter produced by kamma; the material life faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for internal matter produced by kamma. (2)
An external state is a condition for an internal and an external state by way of faculty condition – external faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates, consciousness and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, external faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates, consciousness, and internal and external matter produced by kamma; the material life faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for internal and external matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-354

Internal and external states are a condition for an internal state by way of faculty condition – at the moment of rebirth, internal and external faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for internal matter produced by kamma; the eye faculty and the faculty of equanimity are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for eye-consciousness… the body faculty and the faculty of pleasure… the body faculty and the faculty of pain are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for body-consciousness. (1)
Internal and external states are a condition for an external state by way of faculty condition – internal and external faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, internal and external faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and external matter produced by kamma; the eye faculty and the faculty of equanimity are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body faculty and the faculty of pleasure… the body faculty and the faculty of pain are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness. (2)
Internal and external states are a condition for an internal and an external state by way of faculty condition – at the moment of rebirth, internal and external faculties are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and internal and external matter produced by kamma; the eye faculty and the faculty of equanimity are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the body faculty and…. (3) ^7-32-355

####### *The Jhāna Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-282-11-0

An external state is a condition for an external state by way of jhāna condition… three… by way of path condition… three… by way of association condition… five. ^7-32-356

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-3-1-9-282-12-0

An internal state is a condition for an internal state by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, consciousness is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for internal matter produced by kamma. Pre-arising – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for body-consciousness. Post-arising – post-arising internal aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this internal body that arose earlier. (1)
An internal state is a condition for an external state by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – internal aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Pre-arising – the eye base for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness. Post-arising – post-arising internal aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this external body that arose earlier. (2)
An internal state is a condition for an internal and an external state by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, internal aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for internal and external matter produced by kamma. Pre-arising – the eye base for eye-consciousness and the associated aggregates… the body base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for body-consciousness and the associated aggregates. Post-arising – post-arising internal aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this internal and external body that arose earlier. (3) ^7-32-357

An external state is a condition for an external state by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – external aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… the aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the physical base; the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates. Pre-arising – the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the external aggregates. Post-arising – post-arising external aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this external body that arose earlier. (1)
An external state is a condition for an internal state by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, external aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for internal matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness. Pre-arising – the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for consciousness. Post-arising – post-arising external aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this internal body that arose earlier. (2)
An external state is a condition for an internal and an external state by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-358

Internal and external states are a condition for an internal state by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, internal and external aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for internal matter produced by kamma. Post-arising – post-arising… (abbreviated). (1)
Internal and external states are a condition for an external state by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (2)
Internal and external states are a condition for an internal and an external state by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, internal and external aggregates… (abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-359

####### *The Presence Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-282-13-0

An internal state is a condition for an internal state by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, consciousness is a condition, by way of presence condition, for internal matter produced by kamma. Pre-arising – the eye… the body as impermanent… (the same as pre-arising, without difference). Post-arising (to be worked out in the same way as post-arising). (1)
An internal state is a condition for an external state by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – co-arising internal aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness (abbreviated). (2)
(Here, for the presence condition, in every place the co-arising is the same as in the Condition Section. The pre-arising is the same as pre-arising. The post-arising should be worked out the same as post-arising, without difference.)
An internal state is a condition for an internal and an external state by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-360

An external state is a condition for an external state by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (everything should be expanded). (1)
An external state is a condition for an internal state by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). (2)
An external state is a condition for an internal and an external state by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-361

Internal and external states are a condition for an internal state by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base, the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for eye-consciousness… the aggregates accompanied by body-consciousness and… at the moment of rebirth, internal and external aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for internal matter produced by kamma. Pre-arising – the eye base and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for consciousness… the body base and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for consciousness; the visible-form base and the eye base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base and the body base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for body-consciousness. Post-arising – post-arising internal and external aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this internal body that arose earlier. Post-arising – post-arising internal and external aggregates and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this internal body. Post-arising – post-arising internal and external aggregates and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for internal matter produced by kamma. (1)
Internal and external states are a condition for an external state by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness, the eye base and eye-consciousness are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates… (the same as the section on the co-arising condition, without difference, just like the first course. All the items should be analysed by the method of the first combination). (2)
Internal and external states are a condition for an internal and an external state by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two aggregates and eye-consciousness (abbreviated. All the items should be analysed by the method of the first combination). (3)
A condition by way of absence condition… by way of disappearance condition… by way of non-disappearance condition. ^7-32-362

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-283-0

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-284-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-284-1-0

Root: three, object: nine, predominance: nine, proximity: nine, contiguity: nine, co-arising: nine, mutuality: five, support: nine, strong-support: nine, pre-arising: nine, post-arising: nine, repetition: nine, kamma: three, result: nine, nutriment: nine, faculty: nine, jhāna: three, path: three, association: five, dissociation: nine, presence: nine, absence: nine, disappearance: nine, non-disappearance: nine.
The positive method. ^7-32-363

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-3-1-9-284-2-0

An internal state is a condition for an internal state by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition. (1)
An internal state is a condition for an external state by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition. (2)
An internal state is a condition for an internal and an external state by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition. (3) ^7-32-364

An external state is a condition for an external state by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. (1)

(2)
