###### The Analysis Section ^7-3-1-9-313-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-314-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-315-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-315-1-0

A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of root condition – roots that are not derived are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and not-derived matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a derived state by way of root condition – roots that are not derived are a condition, by way of root condition, for derived matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a derived state and a state that is not derived by way of root condition – roots that are not derived are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and derived and not-derived matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-32-394

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-315-2-0

A derived state is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of object condition – the eye… the body… visible forms… tastes, one sees the physical base with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the taste base is a condition, by way of object condition, for tongue-consciousness; derived aggregates are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (1)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of object condition – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust arises… distress arises; earlier… having emerged from jhāna, jhāna… having emerged from the path, noble ones review the path, the fruit… nibbāna… Nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path, for the fruit and for adverting. Noble ones review abandoned defilements, they review suppressed defilements… earlier… they see the tangible base and the aggregates that are not derived with insight as impermanent… distress arises; by knowledge of others' minds they know the consciousness of one endowed with a state that is not derived; the base of infinite space for the base of infinite consciousness… the base of nothingness for the base of neither perception nor non-perception… the tangible base is a condition, by way of object condition, for body-consciousness; aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (1) ^7-32-395

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-3-1-9-315-3-0

A derived state is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – the eye… the body… visible forms… tastes, the physical base, giving it weight, one enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. (1)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, giving it weight one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Good deeds done earlier… having emerged from jhāna, jhāna… having emerged from the path, giving the path weight… the fruit… giving nibbāna weight, they review it. Nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path, for the fruit; giving weight to the tangible base and the aggregates that are not derived, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is not derived is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and not-derived matter produced by consciousness. (1)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a derived state by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is not derived is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for derived matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a derived state and a state that is not derived by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is not derived is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and derived and not-derived matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-32-396

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-315-4-0

A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates that are not derived; conformity for change-of-lineage… is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for attainment of fruition. (1)
A condition by way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition (the same as the Dependence Section)… by way of mutuality condition (the same as the Dependence Section)… by way of support condition (the same as the support condition in the Condition Section). ^7-32-397

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-3-1-9-315-5-0

A derived state is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on excellence of the eye… the body… excellence of complexion… excellence of sound… excellence of odour… excellence of taste… food, one gives a gift… causes a split in the monastic community. Excellence of the eye… the body… excellence of complexion… excellence of sound… excellence of odour… excellence of taste… food is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one gives a gift… produces an attainment, nurtures conceit, takes up wrong view; virtue… wisdom… lust… longing… bodily pleasure… bodily pain… climate… relying on lodging, one gives a gift… causes a split in the monastic community; faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for the attainment of fruition. (1) ^7-32-398

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-3-1-9-315-6-0

A derived state is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… one sees the physical base with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the taste base for tongue-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates that are not derived. (1)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of pre-arising condition. Pre-arising object – one sees tangible objects with insight as impermanent… distress arises; the tangible base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for body-consciousness. (1)
A derived state and a state that is not derived are a condition for a state that is not derived by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. The tangible base and the body base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for body-consciousness; the tangible base and the physical base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates that are not derived. (1) ^7-32-399

####### *The Post-arising and Repetition Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-315-7-0

A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of post-arising condition – post-arising aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of post-arising condition, for this not-derived body that arose earlier. (The base should be asked) post-arising aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of post-arising condition, for this derived body that arose earlier. (The base should be asked) post-arising aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of post-arising condition, for this derived and not-derived body that arose earlier. (3)
A condition by way of repetition condition… one. ^7-32-400

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-3-1-9-315-8-0

A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition that is not derived is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and not-derived matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition that is not derived is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates and not-derived matter produced by kamma. (1)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a derived state by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition that is not derived is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for derived matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition that is not derived is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for derived matter produced by kamma. (2)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a derived state and a state that is not derived by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition that is not derived is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and derived and not-derived matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition that is not derived is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates and derived and not-derived matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-401

####### *The Result Condition* ^7-3-1-9-315-9-0

A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of result condition – one resultant aggregate that is not derived is a condition for three aggregates… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth… three. ^7-32-402

####### *The Nutriment Condition* ^7-3-1-9-315-10-0

A derived state is a condition for a derived state by way of nutriment condition – physical food is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this derived body. (The base should be asked) physical food is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this not-derived body. (The base should be asked) physical food is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this derived and not-derived body. (3)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of nutriment condition – nutriments that are not derived are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and not-derived matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… (three in the series based on a state that is not derived; all three should be worked out at the moment of rebirth). (3) ^7-32-403

####### *The Faculty Condition* ^7-3-1-9-315-11-0

A derived state is a condition for a derived state by way of faculty condition – the material life faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for derived matter produced by kamma. (The base should be worked out) the eye faculty for eye-consciousness… the body faculty for body-consciousness, the material life faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for not-derived matter produced by kamma. (The base should be worked out) the material life faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for derived and not-derived matter produced by kamma. (3)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of faculty condition… three; at the moment of rebirth….
A derived state and a state that is not derived are a condition for a state that is not derived by way of faculty condition – the eye faculty and eye-consciousness are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the aggregates accompanied by eye-consciousness… the body faculty and…. ^7-32-404

####### *The Jhāna Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-315-12-0

A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of jhāna condition… three, by way of path condition… three; at the moment of rebirth… by way of association condition… one. ^7-32-405

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-3-1-9-315-13-0

A derived state is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates that are not derived. Pre-arising – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base… the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates that are not derived. (1)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for not-derived matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for not-derived matter produced by kamma. Post-arising – post-arising aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this not-derived body that arose earlier. (1)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a derived state by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for derived matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Post-arising – post-arising aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this derived body that arose earlier. (2)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a derived state and a state that is not derived by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for derived and not-derived matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Post-arising – post-arising aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this derived and not-derived body that arose earlier. (3) ^7-32-406

####### *The Presence Condition* ^7-3-1-9-315-14-0

A derived state is a condition for a derived state by way of presence condition – nutriment, faculty. Physical food is a condition, by way of presence condition, for this derived body; the material life faculty is a condition, by way of presence condition, for derived matter produced by kamma. (1)
A derived state is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates that are not derived. Pre-arising – the eye as impermanent… (abbreviated. The same as pre-arising, without difference). Physical food is a condition, by way of presence condition, for this not-derived body; the material life faculty is a condition, by way of presence condition, for not-derived matter produced by kamma. (2)
A derived state is a condition for a derived state and a state that is not derived by way of presence condition – nutriment, faculty. Physical food is a condition, by way of presence condition, for this derived and not-derived body; the material life faculty is a condition, by way of presence condition, for derived and not-derived matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-32-407

A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate that is not derived is a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness that is not derived… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth… one great element is a condition, by way of presence condition, for three great elements… two great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for two great elements (as far as the non-percipient beings). Pre-arising – tangibles as impermanent… distress arises. The tangible base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for body-consciousness. Post-arising – aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that arose earlier and is not derived. (1)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is derived by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness that is derived; at the moment of rebirth…. Post-arising – aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that arose earlier and is derived. (2)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is derived and a state that is not derived by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate that is not derived is a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness that is derived and not derived; two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. Post-arising – aggregates that are not derived are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that arose earlier, both that which is derived and that which is not derived. (3) ^7-32-408

A state that is derived and a state that is not derived are a condition for a state that is derived by way of presence condition – post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Post-arising – aggregates that are not derived and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that is derived. Post-arising – aggregates that are not derived and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma that is derived. (1)
A state that is derived and a state that is not derived are a condition for a state that is not derived by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – one aggregate accompanied by eye-consciousness and the eye base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and… one aggregate that is not derived and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth, one aggregate that is not derived and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and…. Pre-arising – the tangible base and the body base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for body-consciousness. The tangible base and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates that are not derived. Post-arising – aggregates that are not derived and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that is not derived. Post-arising – aggregates that are not derived and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma that is not derived. (2)
A state that is derived and a state that is not derived are a condition for a state that is derived and a state that is not derived by way of presence condition – post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Post-arising – aggregates that are not derived and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that is derived and this body that is not derived. Post-arising – aggregates that are not derived and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma that is derived and not derived. (3) ^7-32-409

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-316-0

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-317-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-317-1-0

Root: three, object: two, predominance: four, proximity: one, contiguity: one, co-arising: five, mutuality: five, support: five, strong-support: two, pre-arising: three, post-arising: three, repetition: one, kamma: three, result: three, nutriment: six, faculty: seven, jhāna: three, path: three, association: one, dissociation: four, presence: nine, absence: one, disappearance: one, non-disappearance: nine.
The positive method. ^7-32-410

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-3-1-9-317-2-0

A state that is derived is a condition for a state that is derived by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. (1)
A state that is derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. (2)
A state that is derived is a condition for a state that is derived and a state that is not derived by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. (3) ^7-32-411

A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is not derived by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. (1)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is derived by way of co-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. (2)
A state that is not derived is a condition for a state that is derived and a state that is not derived by way of co-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. (3)
A state that is derived and a state that is not derived are a condition for a state that is derived: post-arising, nutriment, faculty. (1)
A state that is derived and a state that is not derived are a condition for a state that is not derived: co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. (2)
A state that is derived and a state that is not derived are a condition for a state that is derived and a state that is not derived: post-arising, nutriment, faculty. (3) ^7-32-412

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-319-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-319-1-0

Not-root: nine, not-object: nine (nine in every case), not-association: nine, not-dissociation: six, not-presence: four, not-absence: nine, not-disappearance: nine, not-non-disappearance: four. ^7-32-413

###### Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-320-0

With root as condition – not-object: three, not-predominance: three, not-proximity: three, not-contiguity: three, not-mutuality: three, not-strong-support: three (three in every case), not-association: three, not-dissociation: one, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-32-414

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-321-0

With not-root as condition – object: two, predominance: four… (the positive list is to be worked out)… non-disappearance: nine.
The Derived Pair is finished. ^7-32-415

###### The Clung-to Pair ^7-3-1-9-322-0

###### The Dependence Section ^7-3-1-9-323-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-324-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-325-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-325-1-0

Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate clung-to, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate clung-to, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma… dependent on two aggregates… dependent on the aggregates, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates; one great element… dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a state clung-to, a state not clung-to arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates clung-to, matter produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to and a state not clung-to arise because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate clung-to, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates…. (3)
Dependent on a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate not clung-to, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates… dependent on one great element…; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a state clung-to and a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates clung-to and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-32-416

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-325-2-0

Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises because of the object condition – dependent on one aggregate clung-to, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates. (1)
Dependent on a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises because of the object condition – dependent on one aggregate not clung-to, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates…. (1) ^7-32-417

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-3-1-9-325-3-0

Dependent on a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises because of the predominance condition – dependent on one aggregate not clung-to, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates… dependent on one great element…; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness and derived matter (abbreviated). ^7-32-418

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-326-0

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-327-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-327-1-0

Root: five, object: two, predominance: one, proximity: two, contiguity: two, co-arising: five, mutuality: two, support: five, strong-support: two, pre-arising: two, repetition: one, kamma: five, result: five, nutriment: five, faculty: five, jhāna: five, path: five, association: two, dissociation: five, presence: five, absence: two, disappearance: two, non-disappearance: five. ^7-32-419

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-328-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-329-0

####### *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-329-1-0

Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate clung-to that is without roots, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on one aggregate clung-to, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma… dependent on two aggregates… dependent on the aggregates, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates; dependent on one great element…; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; among non-percipient beings, dependent on one great element…; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a state clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates clung-to that are without roots, matter produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to and a state not clung-to arise not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate clung-to that is without roots, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates…. (3) ^7-32-420

Dependent on a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate not clung-to that is without roots, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates… one great element…; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness and derived matter. External… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature, dependent on one great element…; dependent on the great elements, derived matter. Dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
Dependent on a state clung-to and a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates clung-to that are without roots and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-32-421

####### *The Not-Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-329-2-0

Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises not because of the object condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates clung-to, matter produced by kamma; dependent on the aggregates, the physical base; one great element…; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. Among non-percipient beings, dependent on one great element…; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a state clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the object condition – dependent on the aggregates clung-to, matter produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the object condition – dependent on the aggregates not clung-to, matter produced by consciousness; one great element…; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature, one great element…; dependent on the great elements, derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a state clung-to and a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the object condition – dependent on the aggregates clung-to and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-32-422

####### *The Not-Predominance Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-329-3-0

Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises not because of the predominance condition… not because of the proximity condition… not because of the contiguity condition… not because of the mutuality condition… not because of the strong-support condition. ^7-32-423

####### *The Not-Pre-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-329-4-0

Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises not because of the pre-arising condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one aggregate clung-to, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate clung-to, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma… dependent on two aggregates… (as far as the non-percipient beings). (1)
Dependent on a state clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the pre-arising condition – dependent on the aggregates clung-to, matter produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the pre-arising condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one aggregate not clung-to, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates…; dependent on the aggregates not clung-to, matter produced by consciousness; one great element… (as far as the non-percipient beings). (1)
Dependent on a state clung-to and a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the pre-arising condition – dependent on the aggregates clung-to and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness… not because of the post-arising condition… not because of the repetition condition. (1) ^7-32-424

####### *The Not-Kamma Condition* ^7-3-1-9-329-5-0

Dependent on a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the kamma condition – dependent on the aggregates not clung-to, volition not clung-to… external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature…; dependent on the great elements, derived matter. (1) ^7-32-425

####### *The Not-Result Condition* ^7-3-1-9-329-6-0

Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises not because of the result condition – among non-percipient beings, one great element…; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma and derived matter. (1)
Dependent on a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the result condition – dependent on one aggregate not clung-to, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates… one great element… (as far as produced by temperature). ^7-32-426

####### *The Not-Nutriment Condition* ^7-3-1-9-329-7-0

Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises not because of the nutriment condition – among non-percipient beings, one great element…. (1)
Dependent on a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the nutriment condition – external… produced by temperature…. (1) ^7-32-427

####### *The Not-Faculty Condition* ^7-3-1-9-329-8-0

Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises not because of the faculty condition – among non-percipient beings, dependent on the great elements, the material life faculty. (1)
Dependent on a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the faculty condition – external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature…. (1) ^7-32-428

####### *The Not-Jhāna Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-329-9-0

Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises not because of the jhāna condition – dependent on one aggregate accompanied by the five kinds of sense-consciousness, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… among non-percipient beings….
Dependent on a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the jhāna condition – external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature….
Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises not because of the path condition (the same as the not-root condition; there is no delusion)… not because of the association condition. ^7-32-429

####### *The Not-Dissociation Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-329-10-0

Dependent on a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises not because of the dissociation condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one aggregate clung-to, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… among non-percipient beings, dependent on one great element….
Dependent on a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the dissociation condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one aggregate not clung-to, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature…. Not because of the absence condition… not because of the disappearance condition. ^7-32-430

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-331-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-331-1-0

Not-root: five, not-object: four, not-predominance: five, not-proximity: four, not-contiguity: four, not-mutuality: four, not-strong-support: four, not-pre-arising: four, not-post-arising: five, not-repetition: five, not-kamma: one, not-result: two, not-nutriment: two, not-faculty: two, not-jhāna: two, not-path: five, not-association: four, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: four, not-disappearance: four. ^7-32-431

###### Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-332-0

With root as condition – not-object: four, not-predominance: five… not-pre-arising: four, not-post-arising: five, not-repetition: five, not-kamma: one, not-result: one… not-association: four, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: four, not-disappearance: four. ^7-32-432

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-333-0

With not-root as condition – object: two, proximity: two, contiguity: two, co-arising: five, mutuality: two, support: five, strong-support: two, pre-arising: two, repetition: one, kamma: five, result: five… path: one, association: two… non-disappearance: five. ^7-32-433

###### The Co-arising Section ^7-3-1-9-334-0

(The co-arising section is the same as the dependence section.) ^7-U406

###### The Condition Section ^7-3-1-9-335-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-336-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-337-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-337-1-0

With a state clung-to as condition, a state clung-to arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate clung-to as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, with one aggregate clung-to as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma… with two aggregates… with the aggregates as condition, the physical base; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates; one great element… with the great elements as condition, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates clung-to. (1)
With a state clung-to as condition, a state not clung-to arises because of the root condition – with the aggregates clung-to as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates not clung-to. (2)
With a state clung-to as condition, a state clung-to and a state not clung-to arise because of the root condition – with one aggregate clung-to as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates…. (3)
With a state not clung-to as condition, a state not clung-to arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate not clung-to as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates… one great element…; with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness and derived matter. (1)
With a state clung-to and a state not clung-to as condition, a state not clung-to arises because of the root condition – with the aggregates clung-to and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with one aggregate not clung-to and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates and…. (1) ^7-32-434

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-337-2-0

With a state clung-to as condition, a state clung-to arises because of the object condition – with one aggregate clung-to as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates; with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… the body base…; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates clung-to. (1)
With a state clung-to as condition, a state not clung-to arises because of the object condition – with the physical base as condition, the aggregates not clung-to. (2)
With a state not clung-to as condition, a state not clung-to arises because of the object condition – with one aggregate not clung-to as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates…. (1)
With a state clung-to and a state not clung-to as condition, a state not clung-to arises because of the object condition – with one aggregate not clung-to and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates and…. (1) ^7-32-435

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-3-1-9-337-3-0

With a state clung-to as condition, a state not clung-to arises because of the predominance condition – with the physical base as condition, the aggregates not clung-to. (1)
With a state not clung-to as condition, a state not clung-to arises because of the predominance condition – with one aggregate not clung-to as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates… one great element…; with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness and derived matter. (1)
With a state clung-to and a state not clung-to as condition, a state not clung-to arises because of the predominance condition – with one aggregate not clung-to and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates and…. (1) (Abbreviated.) ^7-32-436

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-338-0

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-339-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-339-1-0

Root: five, object: four, predominance: three, proximity: four, contiguity: four, co-arising: five, mutuality: four, support: five, strong-support: four, pre-arising: four, repetition: three, kamma: five, result: five… non-disappearance: five. ^7-32-437

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-340-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-341-0

####### *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-341-1-0

With a state clung-to as condition, a state clung-to arises not because of the root condition – with one aggregate clung-to that is without roots as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots…; with the aggregates as condition, the physical base; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates; one great element…; with the great elements as condition, matter produced by kamma and derived matter; among non-percipient beings, one great element…; with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… the body base…; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates clung-to that are without roots. (1)
With a state clung-to as condition, a state not clung-to arises not because of the root condition – with the aggregates clung-to that are without roots as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates not clung-to that are without roots; with the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (2)
With a state clung-to as condition, a state clung-to and a state not clung-to arise not because of the root condition – with one aggregate clung-to that is without roots as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates…. (3) ^7-32-438

With a state not clung-to as condition, a state not clung-to arises not because of the root condition – with one aggregate not clung-to that is without roots as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates… one great element…; external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature…; with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
With a state clung-to and a state not clung-to as condition, a state not clung-to arises not because of the root condition – with the aggregates clung-to that are without roots and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with one aggregate not clung-to that is without roots and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates and… with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness and the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1) (Abbreviated.) ^7-32-439

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-342-0

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-343-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-343-1-0

Not-root: five, not-object: four, not-predominance: five, not-proximity: four, not-contiguity: four, not-mutuality: four, not-strong-support: four, not-pre-arising: four, not-post-arising: five, not-repetition: five, not-kamma: three, not-result: four, not-nutriment: two, not-faculty: two, not-jhāna: two, not-path: five, not-association: four, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: four, not-disappearance: four. ^7-32-440

###### Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-344-0

With root as condition – not-object: four, not-predominance: five… not-pre-arising: four, not-post-arising: five, not-repetition: five, not-kamma: three, not-result: three… not-association: four, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: four, not-disappearance: four. ^7-32-441

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-345-0

With not-root as condition – object: four, proximity: four… path: three… non-disappearance: five. ^7-32-442

###### The Support Section ^7-3-1-9-346-0

(The support section is the same as the condition section.) ^7-U407

###### The Conjoined Section ^7-3-1-9-347-0

####### *1-4. Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-347-1-0

Conjoined with a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate clung-to, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth….
Conjoined with a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate not clung-to, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates…. (1)
Root: two, object: two, predominance: one, proximity: two, contiguity: two, co-arising: two, mutuality: two, support: two, strong-support: two, pre-arising: two, repetition: one, kamma: two, result: two… non-disappearance: two.
The positive method. ^7-32-443

Conjoined with a state clung-to, a state clung-to arises not because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate clung-to that is without roots, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots…. (1)
Conjoined with a state not clung-to, a state not clung-to arises not because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate not clung-to that is without roots, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates… conjoined with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
Not-root: two, not-predominance: two, not-pre-arising: two, not-post-arising: two, not-repetition: two, not-kamma: one, not-result: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: two, not-dissociation: two.
The negative method. ^7-32-444

###### The Association Section ^7-3-1-9-348-0

(In the same way the other two countings and the Association Section should also be worked out.) ^7-U408

###### The Analysis Section ^7-3-1-9-349-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-350-0

###### The Exposition ^7-3-1-9-351-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-3-1-9-351-1-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of root condition – roots clung-to are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, roots clung-to are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. (1)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of root condition – roots clung-to are a condition, by way of root condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of root condition – roots clung-to are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of root condition – roots not clung-to are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-32-445

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-3-1-9-351-2-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of object condition – the eye… the body, visible forms clung-to… odours… tastes… tangibles… the physical base, the aggregates clung-to, seen with insight as impermanent… distress arises; when wholesome and unwholesome states have ceased, a result arises as registration; the visible-form base clung-to is a condition, by way of object condition, for eye-consciousness… the odour base clung-to for nose-consciousness… the tangible base is a condition, by way of object condition, for body-consciousness. (1)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of object condition – the eye… the body, visible forms clung-to… odours… tastes… tangibles… the physical base, the aggregates clung-to, seen with insight as impermanent… distress arises; with the divine eye one sees a visible form clung-to. By knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one endowed with consciousness clung-to; the aggregates clung-to are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (2) ^7-32-446

A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of object condition – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust… distress arises; good deeds done earlier…; jhāna…; having emerged from the path, noble ones review the path, the fruit… nibbāna…; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path, for the fruit and for adverting. Noble ones review abandoned defilements… suppressed defilements… earlier…; visible forms not clung-to… sounds… odours… tastes… tangibles, the aggregates not clung-to, seen with insight as impermanent… distress arises; with the divine eye one sees a visible form not clung-to, with the divine ear element one hears a sound. By knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one endowed with consciousness not clung-to; the base of infinite space…; the base of nothingness…; the aggregates not clung-to are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (1)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of object condition – visible forms not clung-to… sounds… odours… tastes… tangibles, the aggregates not clung-to, seen with insight as impermanent… distress arises; when wholesome and unwholesome states have ceased, a result arises as registration; the wholesome state of the base of infinite space is a condition for the resultant state of the base of infinite consciousness… the wholesome state of the base of nothingness…; the visible-form base not clung-to is a condition, by way of object condition, for eye-consciousness… the sound base…; the tangible base for body-consciousness. (2) ^7-32-447

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-3-1-9-351-3-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – the eye… the body, visible forms clung-to… odours… tastes… tangibles… the physical base, the aggregates clung-to: giving them weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. (1)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, giving it weight one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; earlier…; jhāna…; having emerged from the path, noble ones, giving it weight, review the path; the fruit…; giving nibbāna weight, they review it. Nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path, for the fruit; visible forms not clung-to… sounds… odours… tastes… tangibles, the aggregates not clung-to: giving them weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance not clung-to is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-32-448

####### *The Proximity Condition* ^7-3-1-9-351-4-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of proximity condition – preceding clung-to aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following clung-to aggregates. The five kinds of sense-consciousness are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the resultant mind element. The resultant mind element is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the resultant mind-consciousness element. (1)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of proximity condition – life-continuum is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for adverting; the resultant mind-consciousness element is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the functional mind-consciousness element. (2) ^7-32-449

A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates not clung-to are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates not clung-to; conformity is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for change-of-lineage… for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of proximity condition – adverting is a condition for the five kinds of sense-consciousness…; the aggregates not clung-to are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence… by way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… five (the same as the Dependence Section)… by way of mutuality condition… two (the same as the Dependence Section)… by way of support condition (in the Condition Section, the same as the support condition) five. ^7-32-450

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-3-1-9-351-5-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of strong-support condition – proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – bodily pleasure is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain; bodily pain… clung-to temperature… food is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain; bodily pleasure… bodily pain… temperature… food… is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain. (1)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on bodily pleasure, one gives a gift… causes a split in the monastic community; bodily pain… relying on clung-to temperature… food, one gives a gift… produces an attainment; kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; bodily pleasure… bodily pain… temperature… food is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for longing, for the path, for the attainment of fruition. (2) ^7-32-451

A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one gives a gift, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; virtue… longing… temperature… food… relying on lodging, one gives a gift… causes a split in the monastic community; faith… longing… temperature… food… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for longing, for the path, for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of strong-support condition – proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one torments and tortures oneself, one undergoes suffering rooted in seeking; virtue… longing… temperature… food… relying on lodging, one torments and tortures oneself, one undergoes suffering rooted in seeking; faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain. Wholesome and unwholesome kamma is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for result. (2) ^7-32-452

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-3-1-9-351-6-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the body, visible forms clung-to… odours… tastes… tangibles… the physical base as impermanent… distress arises; when wholesome and unwholesome states have ceased, a result arises as registration; the visible-form base clung-to is a condition for eye-consciousness… the odour base… the tangible base is a condition for body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition for eye-consciousness… the body base is a condition for body-consciousness…; the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates clung-to. (1)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the body, visible forms clung-to… odours… tastes… tangibles, the physical base as impermanent… distress arises; with the divine eye one sees a visible form clung-to. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates not clung-to. (2) ^7-32-453

A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of pre-arising condition. Pre-arising object – visible forms not clung-to… sounds… odours… tastes… tangibles as impermanent… distress arises; with the divine eye one sees a visible form not clung-to, with the divine ear element one hears a sound. (1)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of pre-arising condition. Pre-arising object – visible forms not clung-to… sounds… odours… tastes… tangibles as impermanent… distress arises; when wholesome and unwholesome states have ceased, a result arises as registration; the visible-form base not clung-to is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for eye-consciousness… the sound base…; the tangible base for body-consciousness. (2)
A state clung-to and a state not clung-to are a condition for a state clung-to by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. The visible-form base not clung-to and the eye base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for eye-consciousness… the sound base and the ear base…; the tangible base and the body base for body-consciousness; the visible-form base not clung-to and the physical base… the tangible base and the physical base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates clung-to. (1)
A state clung-to and a state not clung-to are a condition for a state not clung-to by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. The visible-form base not clung-to and the physical base… the tangible base and the physical base are a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates not clung-to. (2) ^7-32-454

####### *The Post-arising and Repetition Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-351-7-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of post-arising condition – post-arising aggregates clung-to are a condition, by way of post-arising condition, for this body clung-to that arose earlier. (1)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of post-arising condition – post-arising aggregates clung-to are a condition, by way of post-arising condition, for this body not clung-to that arose earlier. (2)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated). (3)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated). (1)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated). (2)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated). (3) ^7-32-455

A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of repetition condition… one (abbreviated). ^7-32-456

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-3-1-9-351-8-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of kamma condition – volition clung-to is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth, volition clung-to is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. (1)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of kamma condition – volition clung-to is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of kamma condition – volition clung-to is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of kamma condition – volition not clung-to is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (1)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of kamma condition. Arising at different moments – volition not clung-to is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. (2) ^7-32-457

####### *The Result Condition* ^7-3-1-9-351-9-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of result condition – one aggregate clung-to (abbreviated. In the series with the clung-to state as base: three.).
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of result condition – one resultant aggregate not clung-to is a condition, by way of result condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates…. (1) ^7-32-458

####### *The Nutriment Condition* ^7-3-1-9-351-10-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of nutriment condition – nutriments clung-to are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…; physical food clung-to is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body clung-to. (1)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of nutriment condition – nutriments clung-to are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for matter produced by consciousness; physical food clung-to is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body not clung-to. (2)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of nutriment condition – nutriments clung-to are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; physical food clung-to is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body clung-to and not clung-to. (3) ^7-32-459

A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of nutriment condition – nutriments not clung-to are a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; physical food not clung-to is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body not clung-to. (1)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of nutriment condition – physical food not clung-to is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body clung-to. (2)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of nutriment condition – physical food not clung-to is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body clung-to and not clung-to. (3)
A state clung-to and a state not clung-to are a condition for a state clung-to by way of nutriment condition – physical food, both clung-to and not clung-to, is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body clung-to. (1)
A state clung-to and a state not clung-to are a condition for a state not clung-to by way of nutriment condition – physical food, both clung-to and not clung-to, is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body not clung-to. (2)
A state clung-to and a state not clung-to are a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of nutriment condition – physical food, both clung-to and not clung-to, is a condition, by way of nutriment condition, for this body clung-to and not clung-to. (3) ^7-32-460

####### *The Faculty Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-351-11-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of faculty condition – faculties clung-to are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
At the moment of rebirth, faculties clung-to are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by kamma; the eye faculty is a condition for eye-consciousness… the body faculty for body-consciousness…; the material life faculty is a condition, by way of faculty condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to…. (in the series with the clung-to state as base: three; the material life faculty belongs to the first only, in the others there is none). (3)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of faculty condition – faculties not clung-to are a condition, by way of faculty condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (1)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of jhāna condition… four, by way of path condition… four, by way of association condition… two. ^7-32-461

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-3-1-9-351-12-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, the aggregates clung-to are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by kamma; the aggregates are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the physical base; the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates. Pre-arising – the eye base is a condition for eye-consciousness… the body base is a condition for body-consciousness…; the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates clung-to. Post-arising – the aggregates clung-to are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body clung-to that arose earlier. (1)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – the aggregates clung-to are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Pre-arising – the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates not clung-to. Post-arising – the aggregates clung-to are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body not clung-to that arose earlier. (2)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of dissociation condition. Post-arising – the aggregates clung-to are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body that arose earlier, clung-to and not clung-to. (3) ^7-32-462

A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – the aggregates not clung-to are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Post-arising – the aggregates not clung-to are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body not clung-to that arose earlier. (1)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of dissociation condition. Post-arising – the aggregates not clung-to are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body clung-to that arose earlier. (2)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of dissociation condition. Post-arising – the aggregates not clung-to are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body that arose earlier, clung-to and not clung-to. (3) ^7-32-463

####### *The Presence Condition and the Rest* ^7-3-1-9-351-13-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated. The terms set out should be analysed in full). (1)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment (abbreviated; the terms set out should be expanded. (2)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment (abbreviated; the terms set out should be expanded). (3)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment (abbreviated; the terms set out should be analysed). (1)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of presence condition – pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment. Pre-arising – visible forms not clung-to… sounds… odours… tastes… tangibles as impermanent… distress arises; when wholesome and unwholesome states have ceased, a result arises as registration; the visible-form base not clung-to is a condition for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness…. Post-arising – the aggregates not clung-to are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body clung-to that arose earlier; physical food not clung-to is a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body clung-to. (2)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of presence condition – post-arising, nutriment. Post-arising – the aggregates not clung-to are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that arose earlier, clung-to and not clung-to; physical food not clung-to is a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body clung-to and not clung-to. (3) ^7-32-464

A state clung-to and a state not clung-to are a condition for a state clung-to by way of presence condition – pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Pre-arising – the visible-form base not clung-to and the eye base are a condition for eye-consciousness… the tangible base not clung-to and the body base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for body-consciousness; the visible-form base not clung-to and the physical base… the tangible base and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates clung-to. Post-arising – the aggregates clung-to and physical food not clung-to are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body clung-to. Post-arising – the aggregates not clung-to and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (1)
A state clung-to and a state not clung-to are a condition for a state not clung-to by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment. Co-arising – the aggregates clung-to and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Co-arising – one aggregate not clung-to and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and…. Pre-arising – the visible-form base not clung-to and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates not clung-to… the tangible base and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates not clung-to. Post-arising – the aggregates clung-to and physical food not clung-to are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body not clung-to. (2)
A state clung-to and a state not clung-to are a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of presence condition. Nutriment – physical food, both clung-to and not clung-to, is a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body clung-to and not clung-to. (3)
It is a condition by way of absence condition… by way of disappearance condition… by way of non-disappearance condition. ^7-32-465

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-352-0

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-353-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-353-1-0

Root: four, object: four, predominance: two, proximity: four, contiguity: four, co-arising: five, mutuality: two, support: five, strong-support: four, pre-arising: six, post-arising: six, repetition: one, kamma: five, result: four, nutriment: nine, faculty: four, jhāna: four, path: four, association: two, dissociation: six, presence: nine, absence: four, disappearance: four, non-disappearance: nine. ^7-32-466

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-3-1-9-353-2-0

A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. (1)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of nutriment condition. (2)
A state clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of co-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of nutriment condition. (3) ^7-32-467

A state not clung-to is a condition for a state not clung-to by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of nutriment condition. (1)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to by way of object condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition… by way of nutriment condition. (2)
A state not clung-to is a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of post-arising condition… by way of nutriment condition. (3)
A state clung-to and a state not clung-to are a condition for a state clung-to: pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. (1)
A state clung-to and a state not clung-to are a condition for a state not clung-to: co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment. (2)
A state clung-to and a state not clung-to are a condition for a state clung-to and a state not clung-to by way of nutriment condition. (3) ^7-32-468

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-354-0

###### The Counting ^7-3-1-9-355-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-3-1-9-355-1-0

Not-root: nine, not-object: nine (nine in every case), not-nutriment: eight… not-association: nine, not-dissociation: nine, not-presence: four, not-absence: nine, not-disappearance: nine, not-non-disappearance: four. ^7-32-469

###### Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-356-0

With root as condition – not-object: four… not-mutuality: three, not-strong-support: four, not-association: three, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: four, not-disappearance: four. ^7-32-470

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-3-1-9-357-0

With not-root as condition – object: four, predominance: two (the positive list is to be worked out)… non-disappearance: nine.
The Clung-to Pair is finished.
The Major Intermediate Pairs are finished. ^7-32-471
